44!> Control structure for horizontal viscosity
45type, public :: hor_visc_CS ; private
46 logical :: initialized = .false. !< True if this control structure has been initialized.
47 logical :: Laplacian !< Use a Laplacian horizontal viscosity if true.
48 logical :: biharmonic !< Use a biharmonic horizontal viscosity if true.
49 logical :: debug !< If true, write verbose checksums for debugging purposes.
50 logical :: no_slip !< If true, no slip boundary conditions are used.
51 !! Otherwise free slip boundary conditions are assumed.
52 !! The implementation of the free slip boundary
53 !! conditions on a C-grid is much cleaner than the
54 !! no slip boundary conditions. The use of free slip
55 !! b.c.s is strongly encouraged. The no slip b.c.s
56 !! are not implemented with the biharmonic viscosity.
57 logical :: bound_Kh !< If true, the Laplacian coefficient is locally
58 !! limited to guarantee stability.
59 logical :: EY24_EBT_BS !! If true, use an equivalent barotropic backscatter
60 !! with a stabilizing kill switch in MEKE,
61 !< developed by Yankovsky et al. 2024
62 logical :: bound_Ah !< If true, the biharmonic coefficient is locally
63 !! limited to guarantee stability.
64 real :: Re_Ah !! If nonzero, the biharmonic coefficient is scaled
65 !< so that the biharmonic Reynolds number is equal to this [nondim].
66 real :: bound_coef !< The nondimensional coefficient of the ratio of
67 !! the viscosity bounds to the theoretical maximum
68 !! for stability without considering other terms [nondim].
69 !! The default is 0.8.
70 real :: KS_coef !< A nondimensional coefficient on the biharmonic viscosity that sets the
71 !! kill switch for backscatter. Default is 1.0 [nondim].
72 real :: KS_timescale !< A timescale for computing CFL limit for turning off backscatter [T ~> s].
73 logical :: backscatter_underbound !< If true, the bounds on the biharmonic viscosity are allowed
74 !! to increase where the Laplacian viscosity is negative (due to
75 !! backscatter parameterizations) beyond the largest timestep-dependent
76 !! stable values of biharmonic viscosity when no Laplacian viscosity is
77 !! applied. The default is true for historical reasons, but this option
78 !! probably should not be used as it can lead to numerical instabilities.
79 logical :: Smagorinsky_Kh !< If true, use Smagorinsky nonlinear eddy
80 !! viscosity. KH is the background value.
81 logical :: Smagorinsky_Ah !< If true, use a biharmonic form of Smagorinsky
82 !! nonlinear eddy viscosity. AH is the background.
83 logical :: Leith_Kh !< If true, use 2D Leith nonlinear eddy
84 !! viscosity. KH is the background value.
85 logical :: Modified_Leith !< If true, use extra component of Leith viscosity
86 !! to damp divergent flow. To use, still set Leith_Kh=.TRUE.
87 logical :: use_beta_in_Leith !< If true, includes the beta term in the Leith viscosity
88 logical :: Leith_Ah !< If true, use a biharmonic form of 2D Leith
89 !! nonlinear eddy viscosity. AH is the background.
90 logical :: use_Leithy !< If true, use a biharmonic form of 2D Leith
91 !! nonlinear eddy viscosity with harmonic backscatter.
92 !! Ah is the background. Leithy = Leith+E
93 real :: c_K !< Fraction of energy dissipated by the biharmonic term
94 !! that gets backscattered in the Leith+E scheme. [nondim]
95 logical :: smooth_Ah !< If true (default), then Ah and m_leithy are smoothed.
96 !! This smoothing requires a lot of blocking communication.
97 logical :: taper_leithy !< If true, backscatter coeff is tapered to zero with depth
98 real :: leithy_depth !< If tapering leith+E, taper is applied below this depth [Z ~> m]
99 real :: leithy_width !< If tapering leith+E, backscatter is zero below leithy_depth+leithy_width [Z ~> m]
100 logical :: use_QG_Leith_visc !< If true, use QG Leith nonlinear eddy viscosity.
101 !! KH is the background value.
102 logical :: bound_Coriolis !< If true & SMAGORINSKY_AH is used, the biharmonic
103 !! viscosity is modified to include a term that
104 !! scales quadratically with the velocity shears.
105 logical :: use_Kh_bg_2d !< Read 2d background viscosity from a file.
106 logical :: Kh_bg_2d_bug !< If true, retain an answer-changing horizontal indexing bug
107 !! in setting the corner-point viscosities when USE_KH_BG_2D=True.
108 real :: Kh_bg_min !< The minimum value allowed for Laplacian horizontal
109 !! viscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]. The default is 0.0.
110 logical :: FrictWork_bug !< If true, retain an answer-changing bug in calculating FrictWork,
111 !! which cancels the h in thickness flux and the h at velocity point.
112 logical :: OBC_strain_bug !< If true, recover a bug that specified shear strain option at open
113 !! boundaries cannot be applied.
114 logical :: use_land_mask !< Use the land mask for the computation of thicknesses
115 !! at velocity locations. This eliminates the dependence on
116 !! arbitrary values over land or outside of the domain.
117 !! Default is False to maintain answers with legacy experiments
118 !! but should be changed to True for new experiments.
119 logical :: anisotropic !< If true, allow anisotropic component to the viscosity.
120 logical :: add_LES_viscosity!< If true, adds the viscosity from Smagorinsky and Leith to
121 !! the background viscosity instead of taking the maximum.
122 real :: Kh_aniso !< The anisotropic viscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
123 logical :: dynamic_aniso !< If true, the anisotropic viscosity is recomputed as a function
124 !! of state. This is set depending on ANISOTROPIC_MODE.
125 logical :: res_scale_MEKE !< If true, the viscosity contribution from MEKE is scaled by
126 !! the resolution function.
127 logical :: use_GME !< If true, use GME backscatter scheme.
128 integer :: nkblock !< The k block size used in horizontal viscosity calculations [nondim].
129 integer :: answer_date !< The vintage of the order of arithmetic and expressions in the
130 !! horizontal viscosity calculations. Values below 20190101 recover
131 !! the answers from the end of 2018, while higher values use updated
132 !! and more robust forms of the same expressions.
133 real :: GME_h0 !< The strength of GME tapers quadratically to zero when the bathymetric
134 !! total water column thickness is less than GME_H0 [H ~> m or kg m-2]
135 real :: GME_efficiency !< The nondimensional prefactor multiplying the GME coefficient [nondim]
136 real :: GME_limiter !< The absolute maximum value the GME coefficient is allowed to take [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
137 real :: min_grid_Kh !< Minimum horizontal Laplacian viscosity used to
138 !! limit the grid Reynolds number [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
139 real :: min_grid_Ah !< Minimun horizontal biharmonic viscosity used to
140 !! limit grid Reynolds number [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1]
141 logical :: use_cont_thick !< If true, thickness at velocity points adopts h[uv] in BT_cont from continuity solver.
142 logical :: use_cont_thick_bug !< If true, retain an answer-changing bug for thickness at velocity points.
143 type(ZB2020_CS) :: ZB2020 !< Zanna-Bolton 2020 control structure.
144 logical :: use_ZB2020 !< If true, use Zanna-Bolton 2020 parameterization.
145 logical :: use_circulation !< If true, use circulation theorem to compute vorticity (for ZB20 or Leith)
146
147 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: Kh_bg_xx
148 !< The background Laplacian viscosity at h points [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
149 !! The actual viscosity may be the larger of this
150 !! viscosity and the Smagorinsky and Leith viscosities.
151 real, allocatable :: Kh_bg_2d(:,:)
152 !< The background Laplacian viscosity at h points [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
153 !! The actual viscosity may be the larger of this
154 !! viscosity and the Smagorinsky and Leith viscosities.
155 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: Ah_bg_xx
156 !< The background biharmonic viscosity at h points [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1].
157 !! The actual viscosity may be the larger of this
158 !! viscosity and the Smagorinsky and Leith viscosities.
159 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: reduction_xx
160 !< The amount by which stresses through h points are reduced
161 !! due to partial barriers [nondim].
162 real, allocatable :: Kh_Max_xx(:,:) !< The maximum permitted Laplacian viscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
163 real, allocatable :: Ah_Max_xx(:,:) !< The maximum permitted biharmonic viscosity [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1].
164 real, allocatable :: Ah_Max_xx_KS(:,:) !< The maximum permitted biharmonic viscosity for
165 !! the kill switch [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1].
166 real, allocatable :: n1n2_h(:,:) !< Factor n1*n2 in the anisotropic direction tensor at h-points [nondim]
167 real, allocatable :: n1n1_m_n2n2_h(:,:) !< Factor n1**2-n2**2 in the anisotropic direction tensor at h-points [nondim]
168 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: &
169 grid_sp_h2, & !< Harmonic mean of the squares of the grid [L2 ~> m2]
170 grid_sp_h3 !< Harmonic mean of the squares of the grid^(3/2) [L3 ~> m3]
171 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEMB_PTR_,NJMEMB_PTR_) :: Kh_bg_xy
172 !< The background Laplacian viscosity at q points [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
173 !! The actual viscosity may be the larger of this
174 !! viscosity and the Smagorinsky and Leith viscosities.
175 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEMB_PTR_,NJMEMB_PTR_) :: Ah_bg_xy
176 !< The background biharmonic viscosity at q points [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1].
177 !! The actual viscosity may be the larger of this
178 !! viscosity and the Smagorinsky and Leith viscosities.
179 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEMB_PTR_,NJMEMB_PTR_) :: reduction_xy
180 !< The amount by which stresses through q points are reduced
181 !! due to partial barriers [nondim].
182 real, allocatable :: Kh_Max_xy(:,:) !< The maximum permitted Laplacian viscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1].
183 real, allocatable :: Ah_Max_xy(:,:) !< The maximum permitted biharmonic viscosity [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1].
184 real, allocatable :: Ah_Max_xy_KS(:,:) !< The maximum permitted biharmonic viscosity for
185 !! the kill switch [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1].
186 real, allocatable :: n1n2_q(:,:) !< Factor n1*n2 in the anisotropic direction tensor at q-points [nondim]
187 real, allocatable :: n1n1_m_n2n2_q(:,:) !< Factor n1**2-n2**2 in the anisotropic direction tensor at q-points [nondim]
188
189 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEM_) :: &
190 dx2h, & !< Pre-calculated dx^2 at h points [L2 ~> m2]
191 dy2h, & !< Pre-calculated dy^2 at h points [L2 ~> m2]
192 dx_dyT, & !< Pre-calculated dx/dy at h points [nondim]
193 dy_dxT !< Pre-calculated dy/dx at h points [nondim]
194 real, allocatable :: Iwts(:,:) !< Pre-calculated 1./sum_5x5(G%mask2dT) [nondim]
195 real, allocatable :: Iwts_u(:,:) !< 1/sum_5x5(G%mask2Cu) [nondim]
196 real, allocatable :: Iwts_v(:,:) !< 1/sum_5x5(G%mask2Cv) [nondim]
197 real, allocatable :: m_const_leithy(:,:) !< Pre-calculated .5*sqrt(c_K)*max{dx,dy} [L ~> m]
198 real, allocatable :: m_leithy_max(:,:) !< Pre-calculated 4./max(dx,dy)^2 at h points [L-2 ~> m-2]
199 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEMB_PTR_,NJMEMB_PTR_) :: &
200 dx2q, & !< Pre-calculated dx^2 at q points [L2 ~> m2]
201 dy2q, & !< Pre-calculated dy^2 at q points [L2 ~> m2]
202 dx_dyBu, & !< Pre-calculated dx/dy at q points [nondim]
203 dy_dxBu !< Pre-calculated dy/dx at q points [nondim]
204 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEMB_PTR_,NJMEM_) :: &
205 Idx2dyCu, & !< 1/(dx^2 dy) at u points [L-3 ~> m-3]
206 Idxdy2u !< 1/(dx dy^2) at u points [L-3 ~> m-3]
207 real ALLOCABLE_, dimension(NIMEM_,NJMEMB_PTR_) :: &
208 Idx2dyCv, & !< 1/(dx^2 dy) at v points [L-3 ~> m-3]
209 Idxdy2v !< 1/(dx dy^2) at v points [L-3 ~> m-3]
210
211 ! The following variables are precalculated time-invariant combinations of
212 ! parameters and metric terms.
213 real, allocatable :: Laplac2_const_xx(:,:) !< Laplacian metric-dependent constants [L2 ~> m2]
214 real, allocatable :: Biharm6_const_xx(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [L6 ~> m6]
215 real, allocatable :: Laplac3_const_xx(:,:) !< Laplacian metric-dependent constants [L3 ~> m3]
216 real, allocatable :: Biharm_const_xx(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [L4 ~> m4]
217 real, allocatable :: Biharm_const2_xx(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [T L4 ~> s m4]
218 real, allocatable :: Re_Ah_const_xx(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [L3 ~> m3]
219
220 real, allocatable :: Laplac2_const_xy(:,:) !< Laplacian metric-dependent constants [L2 ~> m2]
221 real, allocatable :: Biharm6_const_xy(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [L6 ~> m6]
222 real, allocatable :: Laplac3_const_xy(:,:) !< Laplacian metric-dependent constants [L3 ~> m3]
223 real, allocatable :: Biharm_const_xy(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [L4 ~> m4]
224 real, allocatable :: Biharm_const2_xy(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [T L4 ~> s m4]
225 real, allocatable :: Re_Ah_const_xy(:,:) !< Biharmonic metric-dependent constants [L3 ~> m3]
226
227 type(diag_ctrl), pointer :: diag => NULL() !< structure to regulate diagnostics
228
229 ! real, allocatable :: hf_diffu(:,:,:) ! Zonal horizontal viscous acceleleration times
230 ! ! fractional thickness [L T-2 ~> m s-2].

232 ! ! fractional thickness [L T-2 ~> m s-2].
233 ! 3D diagnostics hf_diffu(diffv) are commented because there is no clarity on proper remapping grid option.
234 ! The code is retained for debugging purposes in the future.
235
236 integer :: num_smooth_gme !< number of smoothing passes for the GME fluxes.
237 !>@{

246 integer :: id_Ah_h = -1, id_Ah_q = -1

263end type hor_visc_CS

265contains
266
267!> Calculates the acceleration due to the horizontal viscosity.
268!!
269!! A combination of biharmonic and Laplacian forms can be used. The coefficient
270!! may either be a constant or a shear-dependent form. The biharmonic is
271!! determined by twice taking the divergence of an appropriately defined stress
272!! tensor. The Laplacian is determined by doing so once.
273!!
274!! To work, the following fields must be set outside of the usual
275!! is:ie range before this subroutine is called:
276!! u(is-2:ie+2,js-2:je+2)
277!! v(is-2:ie+2,js-2:je+2)
278!! h(is-1:ie+1,js-1:je+1) or up to h(is-2:ie+2,js-2:je+2) with some Leith options.
27910subroutine horizontal_viscosity(u, v, h, uh, vh, diffu, diffv, MEKE, VarMix, G, GV, US, &
28010 CS, nkblock, tv, dt, OBC, BT, TD, ADp, hu_cont, hv_cont, STOCH)
281 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
282 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure.
283 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
284 intent(in) :: u !< The zonal velocity [L T-1 ~> m s-1].
285 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
286 intent(in) :: v !< The meridional velocity [L T-1 ~> m s-1].
287 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
288 intent(inout) :: h !< Layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2].
289 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
290 intent(in) :: uh !< The zonal volume transport [H L2 T-1 ~> m3 s-1].
291 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
292 intent(in) :: vh !< The meridional volume transport [H L2 T-1 ~> m3 s-1].
293 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
294 intent(out) :: diffu !< Zonal acceleration due to convergence of
295 !! along-coordinate stress tensor [L T-2 ~> m s-2]
296 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
297 intent(out) :: diffv !< Meridional acceleration due to convergence
298 !! of along-coordinate stress tensor [L T-2 ~> m s-2].
299 type(MEKE_type), intent(inout) :: MEKE !< MEKE fields
300 !! related to Mesoscale Eddy Kinetic Energy.
301 type(VarMix_CS), intent(inout) :: VarMix !< Variable mixing control structure
302 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
303 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
304 integer, intent(in) :: nkblock !< The effective k-block size [nondim]
305 type(thermo_var_ptrs), intent(in) :: tv !< A structure pointing to various
306 !! thermodynamic variables
307 real, intent(in) :: dt !< Time increment [T ~> s]
308 type(ocean_OBC_type), optional, pointer :: OBC !< Pointer to an open boundary condition type
309 type(barotropic_CS), optional, intent(in) :: BT !< Barotropic control structure
310 type(thickness_diffuse_CS), optional, intent(in) :: TD !< Thickness diffusion control structure
311 type(accel_diag_ptrs), optional, intent(in) :: ADp !< Acceleration diagnostics
312 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
313 optional, intent(inout) :: hu_cont !< Layer thickness at u-points [H ~> m or kg m-2].
314 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
315 optional, intent(inout) :: hv_cont !< Layer thickness at v-points [H ~> m or kg m-2].
316 type(stochastic_CS), intent(inout), optional :: STOCH !< Stochastic control structure
317
318 ! Local variables
319 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
32010 Del2u, & ! The u-component of the Laplacian of velocity [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
32110 h_u ! Thickness interpolated to u points [H ~> m or kg m-2].
322 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock) :: &
32310 Del2v, & ! The v-component of the Laplacian of velocity [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
32410 h_v ! Thickness interpolated to v points [H ~> m or kg m-2].
325 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)) :: &
32610 dudx_bt, dvdy_bt ! components in the barotropic horizontal tension [T-1 ~> s-1]
327 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
32810 sh_xx, & ! horizontal tension (du/dx - dv/dy) including metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
32910 sh_xx_smooth ! horizontal tension from smoothed velocity including metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
330 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: &
33110 sh_xx_bt ! barotropic horizontal tension (du/dx - dv/dy) including metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
332 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
33310 str_xx, & ! str_xx is the diagonal term in the stress tensor [H L2 T-2 ~> m3 s-2 or kg s-2], but
334 ! at some points in the code it is not yet layer integrated, so is in [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2].
33510 str_xx_GME, & ! smoothed diagonal term in the stress tensor from GME [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
33610 bhstr_xx, & ! A copy of str_xx that only contains the biharmonic contribution [H L2 T-2 ~> m3 s-2 or kg s-2]
33710 FrictWorkIntz, & ! depth integrated energy dissipated by lateral friction [R Z L2 T-3 ~> W m-2]
33810 FrictWorkIntz_bh, & ! depth integrated energy dissipated by biharmonic lateral friction [R Z L2 T-3 ~> W m-2]
33910 grad_vort_mag_h, & ! Magnitude of vorticity gradient at h-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
34010 grad_vort_mag_h_2d, & ! Magnitude of 2d vorticity gradient at h-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
34110 grad_div_mag_h, & ! Magnitude of divergence gradient at h-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
34210 dudx, dvdy ! components in the horizontal tension [T-1 ~> s-1]
343 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: &
34410 GME_effic_h ! The filtered efficiency of the GME terms at h points [nondim]
345 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
34610 m_leithy ! Kh=m_leithy*Ah in Leith+E parameterization [L-2 ~> m-2]
347 real :: Del2vort_h ! Laplacian of vorticity at h-points [L-2 T-1 ~> m-2 s-1]
348
349 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock) :: &
35010 dvdx, dudy, & ! components in the shearing strain [T-1 ~> s-1]
35110 dvdx_smooth, dudy_smooth, & ! components in the shearing strain from smoothed velocity [T-1 ~> s-1]
35210 dDel2vdx, dDel2udy ! Components in the biharmonic equivalent of the shearing strain [L-2 T-1 ~> m-2 s-1]
353 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)) :: &

467 ! Fields evaluated on active layers, used for constructing 3D stress fields
468 ! NOTE: The position of these declarations can impact performance, due to the
469 ! very large number of stack arrays in this function. Move with caution!
470 ! NOTE: Several of these are declared with the memory extent of q-points, but the
471 ! same arrays are also used at h-points to reduce the memory footprint of this
472 ! module, so they should never be used in halo point or checksum calls.

47910 vert_vort_mag_smooth, & ! magnitude of gradient of smoothed vertical vorticity (h or q) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
48010 hrat_min, & ! h_min divided by the thickness at the stress point (h or q) [nondim]
4815 visc_bound_rem ! fraction of overall viscous bounds that remain to be applied (h or q) [nondim]

4865 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec ; nz = GV%ke

4925 inv_PI3 = 1.0/((4.0*atan(1.0))**3)
4935 inv_PI2 = 1.0/((4.0*atan(1.0))**2)

5180 .and. ((.not. CS%OBC_strain_bug) .or. (OBC%strain_config /= OBC_STRAIN_SPECIFIED))

5220 "MOM_hor_visc: Module must be initialized before it is used.")

5315 backscat_subround = 0.0
5325 if (find_FrictWork .and. allocated(MEKE%mom_src) .and. (MEKE%backscatter_Ro_c > 0.0) .and. &
533 (MEKE%backscatter_Ro_Pow /= 0.0)) &
5340 backscat_subround = (1.0e-16/MEKE%backscatter_Ro_c)**(1.0/MEKE%backscatter_Ro_Pow)

5420 call pass_vector(hu_cont, hv_cont, G%domain, To_All+Scalar_Pair, halo=2)

5465 if (VarMix%use_variable_mixing) then

5495 .and. (.not. allocated(VarMix%Res_fn_h) .or. .not. allocated(VarMix%Res_fn_q))) &
550 call MOM_error(FATAL, "MOM_hor_visc: VarMix%Res_fn_h and VarMix%Res_fn_q "//&
5510 "both need to be associated with Resoln_scaled_Kh or RES_SCALE_MEKE_VISC.")
5520 elseif (CS%res_scale_MEKE) then

5540 "RES_SCALE_MEKE_VISC is True.")

5585 if (CS%use_Leithy .or. CS%debug) then

562 endif

5655 if ((CS%Leith_Kh) .or. (CS%Leith_Ah) .or. (CS%use_Leithy)) then
5660 js_vort = js_Kh-2 ; je_vort = Jeq+2 ; is_vort = is_Kh-2 ; ie_vort = Ieq+2
5670 if ((G%isc-G%isd < 3) .or. (G%isc-G%isd < 3)) call MOM_error(FATAL, &
5680 "The minimum halo size is 3 when a Leith viscosity is being used.")
569 else
5705 js_vort = js-2 ; je_vort = Jeq+1 ; is_vort = is-2 ; ie_vort = Ieq+1
571 endif
572
5735 if (CS%use_GME) then
574 call hor_visc_GME_setup(G, GV, US, CS, h, BT, TD, nkblock, &
575 dudx_bt, dvdy_bt, dvdx_bt, dudy_bt, sh_xx_bt, sh_xy_bt, &
576 GME_effic_h, GME_effic_q, KH_u_GME, KH_v_GME, &
5770 GME_coeff_h, GME_coeff_q, str_xx_GME, str_xy_GME)
578 ! NOTE: sh_xx_bt, sh_xy_bt, GME_effic_h, GME_effic_q, KH_u_GME and KH_v_GME
579 ! are computed on the host above and are only read (never written) on the
580 ! device. dudx_bt, dvdy_bt, dvdx_bt and dudy_bt are only used on the
581 ! host (post_data diagnostics), so they are not mapped at all.
582 !$omp target enter data map(to: sh_xx_bt, sh_xy_bt, GME_effic_h, GME_effic_q, KH_u_GME, KH_v_GME)
583 ! NOTE: GME_coeff_h, GME_coeff_q, str_xx_GME and str_xy_GME are written and
584 ! read entirely on the device (aside from the smooth_GME host round-trip
585 ! below), so a bare alloc is enough on entry.
586 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: GME_coeff_h, GME_coeff_q, str_xx_GME, str_xy_GME)
587 endif
588
5895 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
590 ! Smooth the velocity. Right now it happens twice. In the future
591 ! one might make the number of smoothing cycles a user-specified parameter
5920 do k=1,nz
593 ! One call applies the filter twice
5940 u_smooth(:,:,k) = u(:,:,k)
5950 v_smooth(:,:,k) = v(:,:,k)
5960 call smooth_x9_uv(CS, G, u_smooth(:,:,k), v_smooth(:,:,k), zero_land=.false.)
597 enddo
5980 call pass_vector(u_smooth, v_smooth, G%Domain)
599 ! If tapering the backscatter, calculate depths now
6000 if (CS%taper_leithy) then
601 ! allocate zc
6020 allocate(zc(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV))) ; zc(:,:,:) = 0.0
603 ! Convert layer thicknesses to geometric heights so zc is in [Z ~> m]
604 ! to match the units of CS%leithy_depth and CS%leithy_width.
6050 call thickness_to_dz(h, tv, dz, G, GV, US, halo_size=1)
606 ! Compute zc. Not actual cell centers because it starts at 0 rather than at SSH.
6070 do j=js-1,je+1 ; do i=is-1,ie+1
6080 zc(i,j,1) = 0.5 * dz(i,j,1)
609 enddo ; enddo
6100 do k=2,nz ; do j=js-1,je+1 ; do i=is-1,ie+1
6110 zc(i,j,k) = zc(i,j,k-1) + 0.5 * (dz(i,j,k-1) + dz(i,j,k))
612 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
613 !$omp target enter data map(to: zc)
614 endif
615 endif
616
6175 if (CS%use_QG_Leith_visc .and. ((CS%Leith_Kh) .or. (CS%Leith_Ah))) then
6180 call thickness_to_dz(h, tv, dz, G, GV, US, halo_size=2)
619 ! Calculate isopycnal slopes that will be used for some forms of viscosity.
6200 call calc_QG_slopes(h, tv, dt, G, GV, US, slope_x, slope_y, VarMix, OBC)
621 ! If the following halo update is added, the calculations in calc_QG_slopes could work on just
622 ! the computational domains, and some halo updates outside of this routine could be smaller.
623 ! call pass_vector(slope_x, slope_y, G%Domain, halo=2)
624 endif

6265 if (find_FrictWork .and. allocated(MEKE%mom_src)) then
6270 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
6280 MEKE%mom_src(i,j) = 0.
629 enddo ; enddo
630
6310 if (allocated(MEKE%mom_src_bh)) then
6320 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie
6330 MEKE%mom_src_bh(i,j) = 0.

6445 use_Leith = CS%Leith_Kh .or. CS%Leith_Ah .or. CS%use_Leithy

6465 use_Smag = CS%Smagorinsky_Kh .or. CS%Smagorinsky_Ah

648 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: dudx, dudy, dvdx, dvdy, sh_xx, sh_xy)
649 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: h_u, h_v, hq)

653 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Shear_mag) if (use_Smag)

655 ! NOTE: Kh_BS is only needed by Leithy, but is inside more general kernels
656 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Ah) if (CS%biharmonic)
657 ! TODO: Only needed if FrictWork_bh is true, and currently only used on CPU,
658 ! but I do not yet see any benefit to breaking up the calculation.
659 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: bhstr_xx, bhstr_xy) if (CS%biharmonic)
660
661 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: hrat_min) &
662 !$omp if (CS%bound_Kh .or. CS%bound_Ah)
663 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: visc_bound_rem) &
664 !$omp if (CS%bound_Kh .or. CS%bound_Ah)
665 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: sh_xy_q) &
666 !$omp if (CS%id_sh_xy_q > 0)
667
668 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: vert_vort_mag)
669 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: sh_xx_smooth) &
670 !$omp if (CS%use_Leithy .or. CS%biharmonic)
671 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: BS_coeff_h, BS_coeff_q) &

675115 do kstart=1,nz,nkblock
676110 kend = min(kstart+nkblock-1,nz)
677110 kmax = kend-kstart+1
678110 k=kstart
679
680 ! The following are the forms of the horizontal tension and horizontal
681 ! shearing strain advocated by Smagorinsky (1993) and discussed in
682 ! Griffies and Hallberg (2000).
683
684 ! NOTE: There is a ~1% speedup when the tension and shearing loops below
685 ! are fused (presumably due to shared access of Id[xy]C[uv]). However,
686 ! this breaks the center/vertex index case convention, and also evaluates
687 ! the dudx and dvdy terms beyond their valid bounds.
688 ! TODO: Explore methods for retaining both the syntax and speedup.
689
690 ! Calculate horizontal tension
69140150 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=Jsq-1:Jeq+2, i=Isq-1:Ieq+2) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
6927367360 k = kstart + kk - 1
693 dudx(i,j,kk) = CS%DY_dxT(i,j)*((G%IdyCu(I,j) * u(I,j,k)) - &
6947367360 (G%IdyCu(I-1,j) * u(I-1,j,k)))
695 dvdy(i,j,kk) = CS%DX_dyT(i,j)*((G%IdxCv(i,J) * v(i,J,k)) - &
6967367360 (G%IdxCv(i,J-1) * v(i,J-1,k)))
69714774870 sh_xx(i,j,kk) = dudx(i,j,kk) - dvdy(i,j,kk)
698 enddo
699
700 ! Components for the shearing strain
701110 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_vort:je_vort, I=is_vort:ie_vort) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7027307190 k = kstart + kk - 1
7037307190 dvdx(I,J,kk) = CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)*((v(i+1,J,k)*G%IdyCv(i+1,J)) - (v(i,J,k)*G%IdyCv(i,J)))
70414654420 dudy(I,J,kk) = CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)*((u(I,j+1,k)*G%IdxCu(I,j+1)) - (u(I,j,k)*G%IdxCu(I,j)))
705 enddo
706
707110 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
708 ! Calculate horizontal tension from smoothed velocity
7090 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=Jsq:Jeq+1, i=Isq:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k, dudx_smooth, dvdy_smooth))
7100 k = kstart + kk - 1
711 dudx_smooth = CS%DY_dxT(i,j)*((G%IdyCu(I,j) * u_smooth(I,j,k)) - &
7120 (G%IdyCu(I-1,j) * u_smooth(I-1,j,k)))
713 dvdy_smooth = CS%DX_dyT(i,j)*((G%IdxCv(i,J) * v_smooth(i,J,k)) - &
7140 (G%IdxCv(i,J-1) * v_smooth(i,J-1,k)))
7150 sh_xx_smooth(i,j,kk) = dudx_smooth - dvdy_smooth
716 enddo
717
718 ! Components for the shearing strain from smoothed velocity
7190 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7200 k = kstart + kk - 1
721 dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) = CS%DY_dxBu(I,J) * &
7220 ((v_smooth(i+1,J,k)*G%IdyCv(i+1,J)) - (v_smooth(i,J,k)*G%IdyCv(i,J)))
723 dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) = CS%DX_dyBu(I,J) * &
7240 ((u_smooth(I,j+1,k)*G%IdxCu(I,j+1)) - (u_smooth(I,j,k)*G%IdxCu(I,j)))
725 enddo
726 endif ! use Leith+E
727
728110 if (CS%id_normstress > 0) then
7290 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js:je, i=is:ie) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7300 k = kstart + kk - 1
7310 NoSt(i,j,k) = sh_xx(i,j,kk)
732 enddo
733 endif
734
735 ! Interpolate the thicknesses to velocity points.
736 ! The extra wide halos are to accommodate the cross-corner-point projections
737 ! in OBCs, which are not ordinarily be necessary, and might not be necessary
738 ! even with OBCs if the accelerations are zeroed at OBC points, in which
739 ! case the j-loop for h_u could collapse to j=js=1,je+1. -RWH
740110 if (use_cont_huv) then
7410 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-2:je+2, I=Isq-1:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7420 k = kstart + kk - 1
7430 h_u(I,j,kk) = hu_cont(I,j,k)
744 enddo
7450 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=Jsq-1:Jeq+1, i=is-2:ie+2) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7460 k = kstart + kk - 1
7470 h_v(i,J,kk) = hv_cont(i,J,k)
748 enddo
749110 elseif (CS%use_land_mask) then
75040040 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-2:je+2, I=is-2:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7517347120 k = kstart + kk - 1
75214734280 h_u(I,j,kk) = 0.5 * (G%mask2dT(i,j)*h(i,j,k) + G%mask2dT(i+1,j)*h(i+1,j,k))
753 enddo
75440150 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-2:Jeq+1, i=is-2:ie+2) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7557327320 k = kstart + kk - 1
75614694790 h_v(i,J,kk) = 0.5 * (G%mask2dT(i,j)*h(i,j,k) + G%mask2dT(i,j+1)*h(i,j+1,k))
757 enddo
758 else
7590 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-2:je+2, I=is-2:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7600 k = kstart + kk - 1
7610 h_u(I,j,kk) = 0.5 * (h(i,j,k) + h(i+1,j,k))
762 enddo
7630 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-2:Jeq+1, i=is-2:ie+2) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7640 k = kstart + kk - 1
7650 h_v(i,J,kk) = 0.5 * (h(i,j,k) + h(i,j+1,k))
766 enddo
767 endif
768
769 ! Adjust contributions to shearing strain and interpolated values of
770 ! thicknesses on open boundaries.
771110 if (apply_OBC) then ; do n=1,OBC%number_of_segments
7720 J = OBC%segment(n)%HI%JsdB ; I = OBC%segment(n)%HI%IsdB
7730 if (apply_OBC_strain) then
7740 if (OBC%segment(n)%is_N_or_S .and. (J >= Js_vort) .and. (J <= Je_vort)) then
775 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, &
776 I=max(OBC%segment(n)%HI%IsdB,Is_vort):min(OBC%segment(n)%HI%IedB,Ie_vort)) &
7770 DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
7780 k = kstart + kk - 1
7790 select case (OBC%strain_config)
780 case (OBC_STRAIN_ZERO)
7810 dvdx(I,J,kk) = 0. ; dudy(I,J,kk) = 0.
782 case (OBC_STRAIN_FREESLIP)
7830 dudy(I,J,kk) = 0.
784 case (OBC_STRAIN_COMPUTED)
7850 if (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_N) then
786 dudy(I,J,kk) = 2.0*CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)* &
7870 (OBC%segment(n)%tangential_vel(I,J,k) - u(I,j,k))*G%IdxCu(I,j)
788 else
789 dudy(I,J,kk) = 2.0*CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)* &
7900 (u(I,j+1,k) - OBC%segment(n)%tangential_vel(I,J,k))*G%IdxCu(I,j+1)
791 endif
792 case (OBC_STRAIN_SPECIFIED)
7930 if (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_N) then
7940 dudy(I,J,kk) = CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)*OBC%segment(n)%tangential_grad(I,J,k)*G%IdxCu(I,j)*G%dxBu(I,J)
795 else
7960 dudy(I,J,kk) = CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)*OBC%segment(n)%tangential_grad(I,J,k)*G%IdxCu(I,j+1)*G%dxBu(I,J)
797 endif
798 end select
7990 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
8000 dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) = dvdx(I,J,kk)
8010 dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) = dudy(I,J,kk)
802 endif
803 enddo
8040 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%is_E_or_W .and. (I >= is_vort) .and. (I <= ie_vort)) then
805 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, &
806 J=max(OBC%segment(n)%HI%JsdB,js_vort):min(OBC%segment(n)%HI%JedB,je_vort)) &
8070 DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
8080 k = kstart + kk - 1
8090 select case (OBC%strain_config)
810 case (OBC_STRAIN_ZERO)
8110 dvdx(I,J,kk) = 0. ; dudy(I,J,kk) = 0.
812 case (OBC_STRAIN_FREESLIP)
8130 dvdx(I,J,kk) = 0.
814 case (OBC_STRAIN_COMPUTED)
8150 if (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_E) then
816 dvdx(I,J,kk) = 2.0*CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)* &
8170 (OBC%segment(n)%tangential_vel(I,J,k) - v(i,J,k))*G%IdyCv(i,J)
818 else
819 dvdx(I,J,kk) = 2.0*CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)* &
8200 (v(i+1,J,k) - OBC%segment(n)%tangential_vel(I,J,k))*G%IdyCv(i+1,J)
821 endif
822 case (OBC_STRAIN_SPECIFIED)
8230 if (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_E) then
8240 dvdx(I,J,kk) = CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)*OBC%segment(n)%tangential_grad(I,J,k)*G%IdyCv(i,J)*G%dxBu(I,J)
825 else
8260 dvdx(I,J,kk) = CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)*OBC%segment(n)%tangential_grad(I,J,k)*G%IdyCv(i+1,J)*G%dxBu(I,J)
827 endif
828 end select
8290 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
8300 dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) = dvdx(I,J,kk)
8310 dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) = dudy(I,J,kk)
832 endif
833 enddo
834 endif
835 endif
836
8370 if (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_N) then
838 ! There are extra wide halos here to accommodate the cross-corner-point
839 ! OBC projections, but they might not be necessary if the accelerations
840 ! are always zeroed out at OBC points, in which case the i-loop below
841 ! becomes do i=is-1,ie+1. -RWH
8420 if ((J >= js-2) .and. (J <= Jeq+1)) then
843 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, &
8440 i=max(is-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%isd):min(ie+2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%ied)) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
8450 k = kstart + kk - 1
8460 h_v(i,J,kk) = h(i,j,k)
847 enddo
848 endif
8490 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_S) then
8500 if ((J >= js-2) .and. (J <= Jeq+1)) then
851 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, &
8520 i=max(is-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%isd):min(ie+2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%ied)) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
8530 k = kstart + kk - 1
8540 h_v(i,J,kk) = h(i,j+1,k)
855 enddo
856 endif
8570 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_E) then
8580 if ((I >= is-2) .and. (I <= Ieq+1)) then
859 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, &
8600 j=max(js-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jsd):min(je+2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jed)) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
8610 k = kstart + kk - 1
8620 h_u(I,j,kk) = h(i,j,k)
863 enddo
864 endif
8650 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_W) then
8660 if ((I >= is-2) .and. (I <= Ieq+1)) then
867 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, &
8680 j=max(js-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jsd):min(je+2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jed)) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
8690 k = kstart + kk - 1
8700 h_u(I,j,kk) = h(i+1,j,k)
871 enddo
872 endif
873 endif
874 enddo ; endif
875 ! Now project thicknesses across corner points on OBCs.
876110 if (apply_OBC) then ; do n=1,OBC%number_of_segments
8770 J = OBC%segment(n)%HI%JsdB ; I = OBC%segment(n)%HI%IsdB
8780 if (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_N) then
8790 if ((J >= js-2) .and. (J <= je)) then
8800 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, I=max(is-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%IsdB):min(Ieq+1,OBC%segment(n)%HI%IedB))
8810 h_u(I,j+1,kk) = h_u(I,j,kk)
882 enddo
883 endif
8840 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_S) then
8850 if ((J >= js-1) .and. (J <= je+1)) then
8860 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, I=max(is-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%isd):min(Ieq+1,OBC%segment(n)%HI%ied))
8870 h_u(I,j,kk) = h_u(I,j+1,kk)
888 enddo
889 endif
8900 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_E) then
8910 if ((I >= is-2) .and. (I <= ie)) then
8920 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=max(js-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jsd):min(Jeq+1,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jed))
8930 h_v(i+1,J,kk) = h_v(i,J,kk)
894 enddo
895 endif
8960 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%direction == OBC_DIRECTION_W) then
8970 if ((I >= is-1) .and. (I <= ie+1)) then
8980 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=max(js-2,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jsd):min(Jeq+1,OBC%segment(n)%HI%jed))
8990 h_v(i,J,kk) = h_v(i+1,J,kk)
900 enddo
901 endif
902 endif
903 enddo ; endif
904
905 ! Shearing strain (including no-slip boundary conditions at the 2-D land-sea mask).
906 ! dudy and dvdx include modifications at OBCs from above.
907110 if (CS%no_slip) then
9080 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-2:Jeq+1, I=is-2:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
9090 k = kstart + kk - 1
9100 sh_xy(I,J,kk) = (2.0-G%mask2dBu(I,J)) * ( dvdx(I,J,kk) + dudy(I,J,kk) )
9110 if (CS%id_shearstress > 0) ShSt(I,J,k) = sh_xy(I,J,kk)
912 enddo
913 else
91440040 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-2:Jeq+1, I=is-2:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
9157307190 k = kstart + kk - 1
9167307190 sh_xy(I,J,kk) = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * ( dvdx(I,J,kk) + dudy(I,J,kk) )
91714654420 if (CS%id_shearstress > 0) ShSt(I,J,k) = sh_xy(I,J,kk)
918 enddo
919 endif
920
921110 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
922 ! Shearing strain (including no-slip boundary conditions at the 2-D land-sea mask).
923 ! dudy_smooth and dvdx_smooth do not (yet) include modifications at OBCs from above.
9240 if (CS%no_slip) then
9250 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
9260 sh_xy_smooth(I,J,kk) = (2.0-G%mask2dBu(I,J)) * ( dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) + dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) )
927 enddo
928 else
9290 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
9300 sh_xy_smooth(I,J,kk) = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * ( dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) + dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) )
931 enddo
932 endif
933 endif ! use Leith+E
934
935 ! Evaluate Del2u = x.Div(Grad u) and Del2v = y.Div( Grad u)
936110 if (CS%biharmonic) then
93740040 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-1:Jeq+1, I=Isq-1:Ieq+1)
938 Del2u(I,j,kk) = CS%Idx2dyCu(I,j) * ((CS%dx2q(I,J)*sh_xy(I,J,kk)) - (CS%dx2q(I,J-1)*sh_xy(I,J-1,kk))) + &
93914574560 CS%Idxdy2u(I,j) * ((CS%dy2h(i+1,j)*sh_xx(i+1,j,kk)) - (CS%dy2h(i,j)*sh_xx(i,j,kk)))
940 enddo
94139930 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=Jsq-1:Jeq+1, i=is-1:Ieq+1)
942 Del2v(i,J,kk) = CS%Idxdy2v(i,J) * ((CS%dy2q(I,J)*sh_xy(I,J,kk)) - (CS%dy2q(I-1,J)*sh_xy(I-1,J,kk))) - &
94314614050 CS%Idx2dyCv(i,J) * ((CS%dx2h(i,j+1)*sh_xx(i,j+1,kk)) - (CS%dx2h(i,j)*sh_xx(i,j,kk)))
944 enddo
945110 if (apply_OBC) then ; if (OBC%zero_biharmonic) then
9460 do n=1,OBC%number_of_segments
9470 I = OBC%segment(n)%HI%IsdB ; J = OBC%segment(n)%HI%JsdB
9480 if (OBC%segment(n)%is_N_or_S .and. (J >= Jsq-1) .and. (J <= Jeq+1)) then
9490 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, I=OBC%segment(n)%HI%isd:OBC%segment(n)%HI%ied)
9500 Del2v(i,J,kk) = 0.
951 enddo
9520 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%is_E_or_W .and. (I >= Isq-1) .and. (I <= Ieq+1)) then
9530 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=OBC%segment(n)%HI%jsd:OBC%segment(n)%HI%jed)
9540 Del2u(I,j,kk) = 0.
955 enddo
956 endif
957 enddo
958 endif ; endif
959 endif

961 ! Vorticity
962110 if (use_vort_xy) then
9630 if (CS%no_slip) then
9640 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_vort:je_vort, I=is_vort:ie_vort)
9650 vort_xy(I,J,kk) = (2.0-G%mask2dBu(I,J)) * ( dvdx(I,J,kk) - dudy(I,J,kk) )
966 enddo
967 else
9680 if (CS%use_circulation) then
9690 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_vort:je_vort, I=is_vort:ie_vort) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
9700 k = kstart + kk - 1
971 vort_xy(I,J,kk) = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * G%IareaBu(I,J) * ( &
972 ((v(i+1,J,k)*G%dyCv(i+1,J)) - (v(i,J,k)*G%dyCv(i,J))) &
973 - ((u(I,j+1,k)*G%dxCu(I,j+1)) - (u(I,j,k)*G%dxCu(I,j))) &
9740 )
975 enddo
976 else
9770 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_vort:je_vort, I=is_vort:ie_vort)
9780 vort_xy(I,J,kk) = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * ( dvdx(I,J,kk) - dudy(I,J,kk) )
979 enddo
980 endif
981 endif
982 endif
983
984110 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
9850 if (CS%no_slip) then
9860 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh)
9870 vort_xy_smooth(I,J,kk) = (2.0-G%mask2dBu(I,J)) * ( dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) - dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) )
988 enddo
989 else
9900 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh)
9910 vort_xy_smooth(I,J,kk) = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * ( dvdx_smooth(I,J,kk) - dudy_smooth(I,J,kk) )
992 enddo
993 endif
994 endif

997110 if (use_Leith) then
998 call hor_visc_Leith_grad(G, GV, US, CS, VarMix, nkblock, kstart, kmax, &
999 is, ie, js, je, is_Kh, ie_Kh, js_Kh, je_Kh, Ieq, Jeq, &
1000 h, dz, slope_x, slope_y, dudx, dvdy, vort_xy, vort_xy_smooth, &
1001 grad_vort_mag_h, grad_vort_mag_h_2d, grad_div_mag_h, &
1002 grad_vort_mag_q, grad_vort_mag_q_2d, grad_div_mag_q, &
10030 vert_vort_mag_smooth, Del2vort_q)
1004 endif ! CS%Leith_Kh
1005
1006110 if ((CS%Smagorinsky_Kh) .or. (CS%Smagorinsky_Ah)) then
1007110 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(sh_xx_sq, sh_xy_sq))
10087247240 sh_xx_sq = sh_xx(i,j,kk)**2
1009 sh_xy_sq = 0.25 * ( ((sh_xy(I-1,J-1,kk)**2) + (sh_xy(I,J,kk)**2)) &
10107247240 + ((sh_xy(I-1,J,kk)**2) + (sh_xy(I,J-1,kk)**2)) )
101114534410 Shear_mag(i,j,kk) = sqrt(sh_xx_sq + sh_xy_sq)
1012 enddo
1013 endif
1014
1015110 if (CS%bound_Ah .or. CS%bound_Kh) then
1016110 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(k, h_min))
10177247240 k = kstart + kk - 1
10187247240 h_min = min(h_u(I,j,kk), h_u(I-1,j,kk), h_v(i,J,kk), h_v(i,J-1,kk))
101914534410 hrat_min(i,j,kk) = min(1.0, h_min / (h(i,j,k) + h_neglect))
1020 enddo
1021 endif
1022
1023110 if (CS%Laplacian) then
1024 ! Determine the Laplacian viscosity at h points, using the
1025 ! largest value from several parameterizations. Also get
1026 ! the Laplacian component of str_xx.
1027
10280 if ((CS%Leith_Kh) .or. (CS%Leith_Ah) .or. (CS%use_Leithy)) then
10290 if (CS%use_QG_Leith_visc) then
10300 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(grad_vort, grad_vort_qg))
10310 grad_vort = grad_vort_mag_h(i,j,kk) + grad_div_mag_h(i,j,kk)
10320 grad_vort_qg = 3. * grad_vort_mag_h_2d(i,j,kk)
10330 vert_vort_mag(i,j,kk) = min(grad_vort, grad_vort_qg)
1034 enddo
1035 else
10360 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
10370 vert_vort_mag(i,j,kk) = grad_vort_mag_h(i,j,kk) + grad_div_mag_h(i,j,kk)
1038 enddo
1039 endif
1040 endif
1041
1042 ! Static (pre-computed) background viscosity
10430 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
10440 Kh(i,j,kk) = CS%Kh_bg_xx(i,j)
1045 enddo
1046
1047 ! NOTE: The following do-block can be decomposed and vectorized after the
1048 ! stack size has been reduced.
10490 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
10500 if (CS%add_LES_viscosity) then
10510 if (CS%Smagorinsky_Kh) &
10520 Kh(i,j,kk) = Kh(i,j,kk) + CS%Laplac2_const_xx(i,j) * Shear_mag(i,j,kk)
10530 if (CS%Leith_Kh) &
10540 Kh(i,j,kk) = Kh(i,j,kk) + CS%Laplac3_const_xx(i,j) * vert_vort_mag(i,j,kk) * inv_PI3
1055 else
10560 if (CS%Smagorinsky_Kh) &
10570 Kh(i,j,kk) = max(Kh(i,j,kk), CS%Laplac2_const_xx(i,j) * Shear_mag(i,j,kk))
10580 if (CS%Leith_Kh) &
10590 Kh(i,j,kk) = max(Kh(i,j,kk), CS%Laplac3_const_xx(i,j) * vert_vort_mag(i,j,kk) * inv_PI3)
1060 endif
1061 enddo
1062
1063 ! All viscosity contributions above are subject to resolution scaling
1064
10650 if (rescale_Kh) then
1066 !$omp target update from(Kh)
1067 !$omp target update from(VarMix%Res_fn_h)
10680 do kk=1,kmax ; do j=js_Kh,je_Kh ; do i=is_Kh,ie_Kh
10690 Kh(i,j,kk) = VarMix%Res_fn_h(i,j) * Kh(i,j,kk)
1070 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
1071 !$omp target update to(Kh)
1072 endif
1073
1074 ! Place a floor on the viscosity, if desired.
10750 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
10760 Kh(i,j,kk) = max(Kh(i,j,kk), CS%Kh_bg_min)
1077 enddo
1078
10790 if (use_MEKE_Ku .and. .not. CS%EY24_EBT_BS) then
1080 !$omp target update from(Kh)
1081 ! *Add* the MEKE contribution (which might be negative)
10820 if (use_kh_struct) then

1088 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
1089 else
10900 do kk=1,kmax ; do j=js_Kh,je_Kh ; do i=is_Kh,ie_Kh
10910 k = kstart + kk - 1
10920 Kh(i,j,kk) = Kh(i,j,kk) + MEKE%Ku(i,j) * VarMix%BS_struct(i,j,k)
1093 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
1094 endif

1100 enddo ; enddo ; enddo

11030 Kh(i,j,kk) = Kh(i,j,kk) + MEKE%Ku(i,j)
1104 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
1105 endif
1106 endif

1108 endif

11100 if (CS%anisotropic) then
11110 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
1112 ! *Add* the tension component of anisotropic viscosity
11130 Kh(i,j,kk) = Kh(i,j,kk) + CS%Kh_aniso * (1. - CS%n1n2_h(i,j)**2)
1114 enddo
1115 endif
1116
1117 ! Newer method of bounding for stability
11180 if ((CS%bound_Kh) .and. (CS%bound_Ah)) then
11190 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(Kh_max_here))
11200 visc_bound_rem(i,j,kk) = 1.0
11210 Kh_max_here = hrat_min(i,j,kk) * CS%Kh_Max_xx(i,j)
11220 if (Kh(i,j,kk) >= Kh_max_here) then
11230 visc_bound_rem(i,j,kk) = 0.0
11240 Kh(i,j,kk) = Kh_max_here
11250 elseif ((Kh(i,j,kk) > 0.0) .or. (CS%backscatter_underbound .and. (Kh_max_here > 0.0))) then
11260 visc_bound_rem(i,j,kk) = 1.0 - Kh(i,j,kk) / Kh_max_here
1127 endif
1128 enddo
11290 elseif (CS%bound_Kh) then
11300 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
11310 Kh(i,j,kk) = min(Kh(i,j,kk), hrat_min(i,j,kk) * CS%Kh_Max_xx(i,j))
1132 enddo
1133 endif

11370 k = kstart + kk - 1
11380 Kh_h(i,j,k) = Kh(i,j,kk)
1139 enddo

11420 if (CS%id_grid_Re_Kh>0) then

11440 k = kstart + kk - 1
11450 KE = 0.125*(((u(I,j,k)+u(I-1,j,k))**2) + ((v(i,J,k)+v(i,J-1,k))**2))
11460 grid_Kh = max(Kh(i,j,kk), CS%min_grid_Kh)
11470 grid_Re_Kh(i,j,k) = (sqrt(KE) * sqrt(CS%grid_sp_h2(i,j))) / grid_Kh

11510 if (CS%id_div_xx_h>0) then

1174110 if (CS%anisotropic) then

1176 ! Shearing-strain averaged to h-points

1178 ! *Add* the shear-strain contribution to the xx-component of stress
11790 str_xx(i,j,kk) = str_xx(i,j,kk) - CS%Kh_aniso * CS%n1n2_h(i,j) * CS%n1n1_m_n2n2_h(i,j) * local_strain
1180 enddo

1183110 if (CS%biharmonic) then

1289110 if (CS%id_grid_Re_Ah > 0) then

1295 enddo

129839930 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=Jsq:Jeq+1, i=Isq:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k, d_del2u, d_del2v, d_str))
12997247240 k = kstart + kk - 1
13007247240 d_del2u = (G%IdyCu(I,j) * Del2u(I,j,kk)) - (G%IdyCu(I-1,j) * Del2u(I-1,j,kk))
13017247240 d_del2v = (G%IdxCv(i,J) * Del2v(i,J,kk)) - (G%IdxCv(i,J-1) * Del2v(i,J-1,kk))
13027247240 d_str = Ah(i,j,kk) * ((CS%DY_dxT(i,j) * d_del2u) - (CS%DX_dyT(i,j) * d_del2v))
1303
13047247240 str_xx(i,j,kk) = str_xx(i,j,kk) + d_str
1305
13067247240 if (CS%use_Leithy) str_xx(i,j,kk) = str_xx(i,j,kk) &
13070 - Kh_BS(i,j,kk) * sh_xx_smooth(i,j,kk)
1308

131014534410 bhstr_xx(i,j,kk) = d_str * (h(i,j,k) * CS%reduction_xx(i,j))

1320110 if (CS%biharmonic) then

132239820 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
13237187510 dDel2vdx(I,J,kk) = CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)*((Del2v(i+1,J,kk)*G%IdyCv(i+1,J)) - (Del2v(i,J,kk)*G%IdyCv(i,J)))
132414414840 dDel2udy(I,J,kk) = CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)*((Del2u(I,j+1,kk)*G%IdxCu(I,j+1)) - (Del2u(I,j,kk)*G%IdxCu(I,j)))
1325 enddo
1326 ! Adjust contributions to shearing strain on open boundaries.
1327110 if (apply_OBC) then ; if ((OBC%strain_config == OBC_STRAIN_ZERO) .or. &
1328 (OBC%strain_config == OBC_STRAIN_FREESLIP)) then

13350 elseif (OBC%strain_config == OBC_STRAIN_FREESLIP) then
13360 dDel2udy(I,J,kk) = 0.
1337 endif
1338 enddo
13390 elseif (OBC%segment(n)%is_E_or_W .and. (I >= is-1) .and. (I <= Ieq)) then

13410 if (OBC%strain_config == OBC_STRAIN_ZERO) then
13420 dDel2vdx(I,J,kk) = 0. ; dDel2udy(I,J,kk) = 0.
13430 elseif (OBC%strain_config == OBC_STRAIN_FREESLIP) then
13440 dDel2vdx(I,J,kk) = 0.
1345 endif

1347 endif

1352110 if ((CS%Smagorinsky_Kh) .or. (CS%Smagorinsky_Ah)) then
135339820 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq) DO_LOCALITY(local(sh_xy_sq, sh_xx_sq))
13547187510 sh_xy_sq = sh_xy(I,J,kk)**2
1355 sh_xx_sq = 0.25 * ( ((sh_xx(i,j,kk)**2) + (sh_xx(i+1,j+1,kk)**2)) &
13567187510 + ((sh_xx(i,j+1,kk)**2) + (sh_xx(i+1,j,kk)**2)) )
135714414840 Shear_mag(I,J,kk) = sqrt(sh_xy_sq + sh_xx_sq)

136139820 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq) DO_LOCALITY(local(h2uq, h2vq))
13627187510 h2uq = 4.0 * (h_u(I,j,kk) * h_u(I,j+1,kk))
13637187510 h2vq = 4.0 * (h_v(i,J,kk) * h_v(i+1,J,kk))
1364 hq(I,J,kk) = (2.0 * (h2uq * h2vq)) &
136514414840 / (h_neglect3 + (h2uq + h2vq) * ((h_u(I,j,kk) + h_u(I,j+1,kk)) + (h_v(i,J,kk) + h_v(i+1,J,kk))))
1366 enddo

1368110 if (CS%bound_Ah .or. CS%bound_Kh) then
136939820 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq) DO_LOCALITY(local(h_min))
13707187510 h_min = min(h_u(I,j,kk), h_u(I,j+1,kk), h_v(i,J,kk), h_v(i+1,J,kk))
137114414840 hrat_min(I,J,kk) = min(1.0, h_min / (hq(I,J,kk) + h_neglect))
1372 enddo
1373
1374 endif

1376110 if (CS%no_slip) then
13770 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq) DO_LOCALITY(local(hu, hv))
13780 if (CS%no_slip .and. (G%mask2dBu(I,J) < 0.5)) then
13790 if ((G%mask2dCu(I,j) + G%mask2dCu(I,j+1)) + &
1380 (G%mask2dCv(i,J) + G%mask2dCv(i+1,J)) > 0.0) then
1381 ! This is a coastal vorticity point, so modify hq and hrat_min.
1382
13830 hu = G%mask2dCu(I,j) * h_u(I,j,kk) + G%mask2dCu(I,j+1) * h_u(I,j+1,kk)
13840 hv = G%mask2dCv(i,J) * h_v(i,J,kk) + G%mask2dCv(i+1,J) * h_v(i+1,J,kk)
13850 if ((G%mask2dCu(I,j) + G%mask2dCu(I,j+1)) * &
1386 (G%mask2dCv(i,J) + G%mask2dCv(i+1,J)) == 0.0) then
1387 ! Only one of hu and hv is nonzero, so just add them.
13880 hq(I,J,kk) = hu + hv
13890 hrat_min(I,J,kk) = 1.0
1390 else
1391 ! Both hu and hv are nonzero, so take the harmonic mean.
13920 hq(I,J,kk) = 2.0 * (hu * hv) / ((hu + hv) + h_neglect)
13930 hrat_min(I,J,kk) = min(1.0, min(hu, hv) / (hq(I,J,kk) + h_neglect) )
1394 endif
1395 endif
1396 endif
1397 enddo
1398 endif

14050 G, GV, CS%ZB2020, k)
1406 enddo
1407 endif

1409110 if (CS%Laplacian) then
1410 ! Determine the Laplacian viscosity at q points, using the

14140 if ((CS%Leith_Kh) .or. (CS%Leith_Ah)) then

14180 grad_vort_qg = 3. * grad_vort_mag_q_2d(I,J,kk)
14190 vert_vort_mag(I,J,kk) = min(grad_vort, grad_vort_qg)
1420 enddo
1421 else

14230 vert_vort_mag(I,J,kk) = grad_vort_mag_q(I,J,kk) + grad_div_mag_q(I,J,kk)
1424 enddo
1425 endif
1426 endif

1428 ! Static (pre-computed) background viscosity
14290 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
14300 Kh(I,J,kk) = CS%Kh_bg_xy(I,J)
1431 enddo
1432
14330 if (CS%Smagorinsky_Kh) then
14340 if (CS%add_LES_viscosity) then
14350 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)

1437 enddo

14450 if (CS%Leith_Kh) then

14480 Kh(I,J,kk) = Kh(I,J,kk) + CS%Laplac3_const_xy(I,J) * vert_vort_mag(I,J,kk) * inv_PI3 ! Is this right? -AJA
1449 enddo
1450 else

1454 endif
1455 endif
1456
1457 ! All viscosity contributions above are subject to resolution scaling

2018 ! The Rossby number function is g(Ro) = 1/(1+c.Ro^n)
