20865 if (CS%id_sh_xx_h>0) call post_data(CS%id_sh_xx_h, sh_xx_h, CS%diag)
20875 if (CS%id_sh_xy_q>0) call post_data(CS%id_sh_xy_q, sh_xy_q, CS%diag)
20885 if (CS%id_Ah_q>0) call post_data(CS%id_Ah_q, Ah_q, CS%diag)
20895 if (CS%id_Kh_h>0) call post_data(CS%id_Kh_h, Kh_h, CS%diag)
20905 if (CS%id_grid_Re_Kh>0) call post_data(CS%id_grid_Re_Kh, grid_Re_Kh, CS%diag)
20915 if (CS%id_Kh_q>0) call post_data(CS%id_Kh_q, Kh_q, CS%diag)
20925 if (CS%use_GME) then ! post barotropic tension and strain
20930 if (CS%id_GME_coeff_h > 0) call post_data(CS%id_GME_coeff_h, GME_coeff_h, CS%diag)
20940 if (CS%id_GME_coeff_q > 0) call post_data(CS%id_GME_coeff_q, GME_coeff_q, CS%diag)
20950 if (CS%id_FrictWork_GME>0) call post_data(CS%id_FrictWork_GME, FrictWork_GME, CS%diag)
20960 if (CS%id_dudx_bt > 0) call post_data(CS%id_dudx_bt, dudx_bt, CS%diag)
20970 if (CS%id_dvdy_bt > 0) call post_data(CS%id_dvdy_bt, dvdy_bt, CS%diag)
20980 if (CS%id_dudy_bt > 0) call post_data(CS%id_dudy_bt, dudy_bt, CS%diag)
20990 if (CS%id_dvdx_bt > 0) call post_data(CS%id_dvdx_bt, dvdx_bt, CS%diag)
2100 endif
21015 if (CS%EY24_EBT_BS) then
21020 if (CS%id_visc_limit_h>0) call post_data(CS%id_visc_limit_h, visc_limit_h, CS%diag)
21030 if (CS%id_visc_limit_q>0) call post_data(CS%id_visc_limit_q, visc_limit_q, CS%diag)
21040 if (CS%id_visc_limit_h_frac>0) call post_data(CS%id_visc_limit_h_frac, visc_limit_h_frac, CS%diag)
21050 if (CS%id_visc_limit_q_frac>0) call post_data(CS%id_visc_limit_q_frac, visc_limit_q_frac, CS%diag)
21060 if (CS%id_visc_limit_h_flag>0) call post_data(CS%id_visc_limit_h_flag, visc_limit_h_flag, CS%diag)
21070 if (CS%id_visc_limit_q_flag>0) call post_data(CS%id_visc_limit_q_flag, visc_limit_q_flag, CS%diag)
2108 endif
2109
21105 if (CS%EY24_EBT_BS .or. CS%use_leithy) then
2111 !$omp target update from(BS_coeff_h, BS_coeff_q)
21120 if (CS%id_BS_coeff_h>0) call post_data(CS%id_BS_coeff_h, BS_coeff_h, CS%diag)
21130 if (CS%id_BS_coeff_q>0) call post_data(CS%id_BS_coeff_q, BS_coeff_q, CS%diag)
2114 !$omp target exit data map(delete: BS_coeff_h, BS_coeff_q)
2115 endif
2116
21175 if (CS%debug) then
21180 if (CS%Laplacian) then
21190 call hchksum(Kh_h, "Kh_h", G%HI, haloshift=1, unscale=US%L_to_m**2*US%s_to_T)
21200 call Bchksum(Kh_q, "Kh_q", G%HI, haloshift=0, symmetric=.true., unscale=US%L_to_m**2*US%s_to_T)
2121 endif
21220 if (CS%biharmonic) then
21230 call hchksum(Ah_h, "Ah_h", G%HI, haloshift=1, unscale=US%L_to_m**4*US%s_to_T)
21240 call Bchksum(Ah_q, "Ah_q", G%HI, haloshift=0, symmetric=.true., unscale=US%L_to_m**4*US%s_to_T)
2125 endif
2126 endif
2127
2128 !$omp target exit data map(delete: sh_xy_q) &
2129 !$omp if (CS%id_sh_xy_q > 0)
2130
21315 if (CS%id_FrictWorkIntz > 0) then
21320 do j=js,je
21330 do i=is,ie ; FrictWorkIntz(i,j,1) = FrictWork(i,j,1) ; enddo
21340 do k=2,nz ; do i=is,ie
21350 FrictWorkIntz(i,j,1) = FrictWorkIntz(i,j,1) + FrictWork(i,j,k)
2136 enddo ; enddo
2137 enddo
21380 call post_data(CS%id_FrictWorkIntz, FrictWorkIntz(:,:,1), CS%diag)
2139 endif
2140
21415 if (CS%id_FrictWorkIntz_bh > 0) then
21420 do j=js,je
21430 do i=is,ie ; FrictWorkIntz_bh(i,j,1) = FrictWork_bh(i,j,1) ; enddo
21440 do k=2,nz ; do i=is,ie
21450 FrictWorkIntz_bh(i,j,1) = FrictWorkIntz_bh(i,j,1) + FrictWork_bh(i,j,k)
2146 enddo ; enddo
2147 enddo
21480 call post_data(CS%id_FrictWorkIntz_bh, FrictWorkIntz_bh(:,:,1), CS%diag)
2149 endif
2150
21515 if (present(ADp)) then
2152 ! Diagnostics of the fractional thicknesses times momentum budget terms
2153 ! 3D diagnostics of hf_diffu(diffv) are commented because there is no clarity on proper remapping grid option.
2154 ! The code is retained for debugging purposes in the future.
2155 !if (CS%id_hf_diffu > 0) call post_product_u(CS%id_hf_diffu, diffu, ADp%diag_hfrac_u, G, nz, CS%diag)
2156 !if (CS%id_hf_diffv > 0) call post_product_v(CS%id_hf_diffv, diffv, ADp%diag_hfrac_v, G, nz, CS%diag)
2157
2158 ! Diagnostics for thickness-weighted vertically averaged momentum budget terms
21594 if (CS%id_hf_diffu_2d > 0) call post_product_sum_u(CS%id_hf_diffu_2d, diffu, ADp%diag_hfrac_u, G, nz, CS%diag)
21604 if (CS%id_hf_diffv_2d > 0) call post_product_sum_v(CS%id_hf_diffv_2d, diffv, ADp%diag_hfrac_v, G, nz, CS%diag)
2161
2162 ! Diagnostics for the vertical sum of layer thickness x momentum budget terms
21634 if (CS%id_intz_diffu_2d > 0) call post_product_sum_u(CS%id_intz_diffu_2d, diffu, ADp%diag_hu, G, nz, CS%diag)
21644 if (CS%id_intz_diffv_2d > 0) call post_product_sum_v(CS%id_intz_diffv_2d, diffv, ADp%diag_hv, G, nz, CS%diag)
2165
2166 ! Diagnostics for thickness x momentum budget terms
21674 if (CS%id_h_diffu > 0) call post_product_u(CS%id_h_diffu, diffu, ADp%diag_hu, G, nz, CS%diag)
21684 if (CS%id_h_diffv > 0) call post_product_v(CS%id_h_diffv, diffv, ADp%diag_hv, G, nz, CS%diag)
2169
2170 ! Diagnostics for momentum budget terms multiplied by visc_rem_[uv],
21714 if (CS%id_diffu_visc_rem > 0) call post_product_u(CS%id_diffu_visc_rem, diffu, ADp%visc_rem_u, G, nz, CS%diag)
21724 if (CS%id_diffv_visc_rem > 0) call post_product_v(CS%id_diffv_visc_rem, diffv, ADp%visc_rem_v, G, nz, CS%diag)
2173 endif
2174
21755 if (CS%use_ZB2020) then
2176 call ZB2020_lateral_stress(u, v, h, diffu, diffv, G, GV, CS%ZB2020, &
21770 CS%dx2h, CS%dy2h, CS%dx2q, CS%dy2q)
2178 endif
2179
21805 if (allocated(zc)) then
2181 !$omp target exit data map(delete: zc)
21820 deallocate(zc)
2183 endif
218415end subroutine horizontal_viscosity
2185
2186!> Calculates the magnitude of the vorticity and divergence gradients, and the
2187!! Laplacian of vorticity, that are used within horizontal_viscosity by the Leith,
2188!! modified Leith, Leith+E, and QG Leith viscosity schemes. The caller must only
2189!! invoke this routine when CS%Leith_Kh, CS%Leith_Ah, or CS%use_Leithy is true.
21900subroutine hor_visc_Leith_grad(G, GV, US, CS, VarMix, nkblock, kstart, kmax, &
2191 is, ie, js, je, is_Kh, ie_Kh, js_Kh, je_Kh, Ieq, Jeq, &
21920 h, dz, slope_x, slope_y, dudx, dvdy, vort_xy, vort_xy_smooth, &
21930 grad_vort_mag_h, grad_vort_mag_h_2d, grad_div_mag_h, &
21940 grad_vort_mag_q, grad_vort_mag_q_2d, grad_div_mag_q, &
21950 vert_vort_mag_smooth, Del2vort_q)
2196 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
2197 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure.
2198 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
2199 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2200 type(VarMix_CS), intent(inout) :: VarMix !< Variable mixing control structure
2201 integer, intent(in) :: nkblock !< The k-block size used to size the following arrays [nondim]
2202 integer, intent(in) :: kstart !< The first absolute k-layer of the current k-block
2203 integer, intent(in) :: kmax !< The number of active k-layers in the current k-block
2204 integer, intent(in) :: is !< Start i-loop index for the h-point viscosities
2205 integer, intent(in) :: ie !< End i-loop index for the h-point viscosities
2206 integer, intent(in) :: js !< Start j-loop index for the h-point viscosities
2207 integer, intent(in) :: je !< End j-loop index for the h-point viscosities
2208 integer, intent(in) :: is_Kh !< Start i-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2209 integer, intent(in) :: ie_Kh !< End i-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2210 integer, intent(in) :: js_Kh !< Start j-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2211 integer, intent(in) :: je_Kh !< End j-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2212 integer, intent(in) :: Ieq !< The last i-index at q-points
2213 integer, intent(in) :: Jeq !< The last j-index at q-points
2214 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2215 intent(in) :: h !< Layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2].
2216 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2217 intent(in) :: dz !< Height change across layers [Z ~> m]
2218 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), &
2219 intent(inout) :: slope_x !< Isopycnal slope in i-direction [Z L-1 ~> nondim]
2220 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), &
2221 intent(inout) :: slope_y !< Isopycnal slope in j-direction [Z L-1 ~> nondim]
2222 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2223 intent(in) :: dudx !< x-derivative of the horizontal tension [T-1 ~> s-1]
2224 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2225 intent(in) :: dvdy !< y-derivative of the horizontal tension [T-1 ~> s-1]
2226 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2227 intent(in) :: vort_xy !< Vertical vorticity (dv/dx - du/dy) including
2228 !! metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
2229 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2230 intent(in) :: vort_xy_smooth !< Vertical vorticity including metric
2231 !! terms, smoothed [T-1 ~> s-1]
2232 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2233 intent(out) :: grad_vort_mag_h !< Magnitude of vorticity gradient at
2234 !! h-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2235 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2236 intent(out) :: grad_vort_mag_h_2d !< Magnitude of 2d vorticity gradient
2237 !! at h-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2238 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2239 intent(out) :: grad_div_mag_h !< Magnitude of divergence gradient at
2240 !! h-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2241 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2242 intent(out) :: grad_vort_mag_q !< Magnitude of vorticity gradient at
2243 !! q-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2244 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2245 intent(out) :: grad_vort_mag_q_2d !< Magnitude of 2d vorticity gradient
2246 !! at q-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2247 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2248 intent(out) :: grad_div_mag_q !< Magnitude of divergence gradient at
2249 !! q-points [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2250 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2251 intent(out) :: vert_vort_mag_smooth !< Magnitude of gradient of smoothed
2252 !! vertical vorticity (h or q) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2253 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2254 intent(out) :: Del2vort_q !< Laplacian of vorticity at q-points
2255 !! [L-2 T-1 ~> m-2 s-1]
2256 ! Local variables
2257 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock) :: &
22580 vort_xy_dx, & ! x-derivative of vertical vorticity (d/dx(dv/dx - du/dy)) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
22590 vort_xy_dx_smooth, & ! x-derivative of smoothed vertical vorticity [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
22600 div_xx_dy ! y-derivative of horizontal divergence (d/dy(du/dx + dv/dy)) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2261 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
22620 vort_xy_dy, & ! y-derivative of vertical vorticity (d/dy(dv/dx - du/dy)) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
22630 vort_xy_dy_smooth, & ! y-derivative of smoothed vertical vorticity [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
22640 div_xx_dx ! x-derivative of horizontal divergence (d/dx(du/dx + dv/dy)) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2265 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
22660 div_xx ! Estimate of horizontal divergence at h-points [T-1 ~> s-1]
2267 real :: DY_dxBu ! Ratio of meridional over zonal grid spacing at vertices [nondim]
2268 real :: DX_dyBu ! Ratio of zonal over meridional grid spacing at vertices [nondim]
2269 integer :: i, j, k, kk
2270
2271 ! Vorticity gradient
22720 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-2:je_Kh, i=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(DY_dxBu))
22730 DY_dxBu = G%dyBu(I,J) * G%IdxBu(I,J)
22740 vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) = DY_dxBu * ((vort_xy(I,J,kk) * G%IdyCu(I,j)) - (vort_xy(I-1,J,kk) * G%IdyCu(I-1,j)))
2275 enddo
2276
22770 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh-1:je_Kh+1, I=is-2:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(DX_dyBu))
22780 DX_dyBu = G%dxBu(I,J) * G%IdyBu(I,J)
22790 vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) = DX_dyBu * ((vort_xy(I,J,kk) * G%IdxCv(i,J)) - (vort_xy(I,J-1,kk) * G%IdxCv(i,J-1)))
2280 enddo
2281
22820 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
2283 ! Gradient of smoothed vorticity
22840 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(DY_dxBu))
22850 DY_dxBu = G%dyBu(I,J) * G%IdxBu(I,J)
2286 vort_xy_dx_smooth(i,J,kk) = DY_dxBu * &
22870 ((vort_xy_smooth(I,J,kk) * G%IdyCu(I,j)) - (vort_xy_smooth(I-1,J,kk) * G%IdyCu(I-1,j)))
2288 enddo
2289
22900 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(DX_dyBu))
22910 DX_dyBu = G%dxBu(I,J) * G%IdyBu(I,J)
2292 vort_xy_dy_smooth(I,j,kk) = DX_dyBu * &
22930 ((vort_xy_smooth(I,J,kk) * G%IdxCv(i,J)) - (vort_xy_smooth(I,J-1,kk) * G%IdxCv(i,J-1)))
2294 enddo
2295 endif ! If Leithy
2296
2297 ! Laplacian of vorticity
2298 ! if (CS%Leith_Ah .or. CS%use_Leithy) then
22990 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(DY_dxBu, DX_dyBu))
23000 DY_dxBu = G%dyBu(I,J) * G%IdxBu(I,J)
23010 DX_dyBu = G%dxBu(I,J) * G%IdyBu(I,J)
2302
2303 Del2vort_q(I,J,kk) = DY_dxBu * ((vort_xy_dx(i+1,J,kk) * G%IdyCv(i+1,J)) - (vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) * G%IdyCv(i,J))) + &
23040 DX_dyBu * ((vort_xy_dy(I,j+1,kk) * G%IdyCu(I,j+1)) - (vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) * G%IdyCu(I,j)))
2305 enddo
2306 ! endif
2307
23080 if (CS%modified_Leith) then
2309
2310 ! Divergence
23110 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh-1:je_Kh+1, i=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh+1)
23120 div_xx(i,j,kk) = dudx(i,j,kk) + dvdy(i,j,kk)
2313 enddo
2314
2315 ! Divergence gradient
23160 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-1:je+1, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh)
23170 div_xx_dx(I,j,kk) = G%IdxCu(I,j)*(div_xx(i+1,j,kk) - div_xx(i,j,kk))
2318 enddo
23190 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, i=is-1:ie+1)
23200 div_xx_dy(i,J,kk) = G%IdyCv(i,J)*(div_xx(i,j+1,kk) - div_xx(i,j,kk))
2321 enddo
2322
2323 ! Magnitude of divergence gradient
23240 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
2325 grad_div_mag_h(i,j,kk) = sqrt(((0.5*(div_xx_dx(I,j,kk) + div_xx_dx(I-1,j,kk)))**2) + &
23260 ((0.5*(div_xx_dy(i,J,kk) + div_xx_dy(i,J-1,kk)))**2))
2327 enddo
23280 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
2329 grad_div_mag_q(I,J,kk) = sqrt(((0.5*(div_xx_dx(I,j,kk) + div_xx_dx(I,j+1,kk)))**2) + &
23300 ((0.5*(div_xx_dy(i,J,kk) + div_xx_dy(i+1,J,kk)))**2))
2331 enddo
2332
2333 else
2334
23350 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-1:je+1, I=is_Kh-1:ie_Kh)
23360 div_xx_dx(I,j,kk) = 0.0
2337 enddo
23380 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js_Kh-1:je_Kh, i=is-1:ie+1)
23390 div_xx_dy(i,J,kk) = 0.0
2340 enddo
23410 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
23420 grad_div_mag_h(i,j,kk) = 0.0
2343 enddo
23440 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
23450 grad_div_mag_q(I,J,kk) = 0.0
2346 enddo
2347
2348 endif ! CS%modified_Leith
2349
2350 ! Add in beta for the Leith viscosity
23510 if (CS%use_beta_in_Leith) then
23520 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-2:Jeq+1, i=is-1:ie+1)
23530 vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) = vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) + 0.5 * ( G%dF_dx(i,j) + G%dF_dx(i,j+1))
2354 enddo
23550 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js-1:je+1, I=is-2:Ieq+1)
23560 vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) = vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) + 0.5 * ( G%dF_dy(i,j) + G%dF_dy(i+1,j))
2357 enddo
2358 endif ! CS%use_beta_in_Leith
2359
23600 if (CS%use_QG_Leith_visc) then
2361
23620 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
2363 grad_vort_mag_h_2d(i,j,kk) = SQRT(((0.5*(vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) + vort_xy_dx(i,J-1,kk)))**2) + &
23640 ((0.5*(vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) + vort_xy_dy(I-1,j,kk)))**2) )
2365 enddo
23660 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
2367 grad_vort_mag_q_2d(I,J,kk) = SQRT(((0.5*(vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) + vort_xy_dx(i+1,J,kk)))**2) + &
23680 ((0.5*(vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) + vort_xy_dy(I,j+1,kk)))**2) )
2369 enddo
2370
2371 ! This accumulates terms, some of which are in VarMix.
23720 do kk=1,kmax
23730 k = kstart + kk - 1
2374 call calc_QG_Leith_viscosity(VarMix, G, GV, US, h, dz, k, div_xx_dx(:,:,kk), div_xx_dy(:,:,kk), &
23750 slope_x, slope_y, vort_xy_dx(:,:,kk), vort_xy_dy(:,:,kk))
2376 enddo
2377
2378 endif
2379
23800 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
2381 grad_vort_mag_h(i,j,kk) = SQRT(((0.5*(vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) + vort_xy_dx(i,J-1,kk)))**2) + &
23820 ((0.5*(vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) + vort_xy_dy(I-1,j,kk)))**2) )
2383 enddo
23840 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
2385 grad_vort_mag_q(I,J,kk) = SQRT(((0.5*(vort_xy_dx(i,J,kk) + vort_xy_dx(i+1,J,kk)))**2) + &
23860 ((0.5*(vort_xy_dy(I,j,kk) + vort_xy_dy(I,j+1,kk)))**2) )
2387 enddo
2388
23890 if (CS%use_Leithy) then
23900 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
2391 vert_vort_mag_smooth(i,j,kk) = SQRT(((0.5*(vort_xy_dx_smooth(i,J,kk) + &
2392 vort_xy_dx_smooth(i,J-1,kk)))**2) + &
2393 ((0.5*(vort_xy_dy_smooth(I,j,kk) + &
23940 vort_xy_dy_smooth(I-1,j,kk)))**2) )
2395 enddo
2396 endif ! Leithy
2397
23980end subroutine hor_visc_Leith_grad
2399
2400!> Adds the MEKE-based (EY24_EBT_BS) backscatter contribution to the diagonal term of
2401!! the stress tensor at h-points. The caller must only invoke this routine when
2402!! CS%EY24_EBT_BS is true.
24030subroutine hor_visc_backscatter_h(G, CS, MEKE, VarMix, use_kh_struct, nkblock, kstart, kmax, &
24040 Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq, visc_limit_h_flag, sh_xx, str_xx, BS_coeff_h)
2405 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
2406 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2407 type(MEKE_type), intent(in) :: MEKE !< MEKE fields related to Mesoscale Eddy Kinetic Energy.
2408 type(VarMix_CS), intent(in) :: VarMix !< Variable mixing control structure
2409 logical, intent(in) :: use_kh_struct !< If true, shape the backscatter coefficient
2410 !! with VarMix%BS_struct
2411 integer, intent(in) :: nkblock !< The k-block size used to size the following arrays [nondim]
2412 integer, intent(in) :: kstart !< The first absolute k-layer of the current k-block
2413 integer, intent(in) :: kmax !< The number of active k-layers in the current k-block
2414 integer, intent(in) :: Isq !< Start i-loop index for the stress tensor at h-points
2415 integer, intent(in) :: Ieq !< End i-loop index for the stress tensor at h-points
2416 integer, intent(in) :: Jsq !< Start j-loop index for the stress tensor at h-points
2417 integer, intent(in) :: Jeq !< End j-loop index for the stress tensor at h-points
2418 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(G)), &
2419 intent(in) :: visc_limit_h_flag !< determines whether backscatter is shut off [nondim]
2420 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2421 intent(in) :: sh_xx !< horizontal tension (du/dx - dv/dy) including
2422 !! metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
2423 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2424 intent(inout) :: str_xx !< The diagonal term in the stress tensor
2425 !! [H L2 T-2 ~> m3 s-2 or kg s-2]
2426 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(G)), &
2427 intent(inout) :: BS_coeff_h !< A diagnostic array of the backscatter
2428 !! coefficient [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2429 ! Local variables
2430 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
24310 Kh_BS, & ! Laplacian antiviscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
24320 str_xx_BS ! The diagonal term in the stress tensor due to backscatter [H L2 T-2 ~> m3 s-2 or kg s-2]
2433 integer :: i, j, k, kk
2434
24350 do kk=1,kmax ; do j=Jsq,Jeq+1 ; do i=Isq,Ieq+1
24360 k = kstart + kk - 1
24370 if (visc_limit_h_flag(i,j,k) > 0) then
24380 Kh_BS(i,j,kk) = 0.
2439 else
24400 if (use_kh_struct) then
24410 Kh_BS(i,j,kk) = MEKE%Ku(i,j) * VarMix%BS_struct(i,j,k)
2442 else
24430 Kh_BS(i,j,kk) = MEKE%Ku(i,j)
2444 endif
2445 endif
2446 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
2447
24480 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=Jsq:Jeq+1, i=Isq:Ieq+1)
24490 str_xx_BS(i,j,kk) = -Kh_BS(i,j,kk) * sh_xx(i,j,kk)
2450 enddo
2451
24520 if (CS%id_BS_coeff_h>0) then
24530 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=Jsq:Jeq+1, i=Isq:Ieq+1) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
24540 k = kstart + kk - 1
24550 BS_coeff_h(i,j,k) = Kh_BS(i,j,kk)
2456 enddo
2457 endif
2458
24590 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=Jsq:Jeq+1, i=Isq:Ieq+1)
24600 str_xx(i,j,kk) = str_xx(i,j,kk) + str_xx_BS(i,j,kk)
2461 enddo
2462
24630end subroutine hor_visc_backscatter_h
2464
2465!> Adds the MEKE-based (EY24_EBT_BS) backscatter contribution to the cross term of
2466!! the stress tensor at q-points. The caller must only invoke this routine when
2467!! CS%EY24_EBT_BS is true.
24680subroutine hor_visc_backscatter_q(G, GV, CS, MEKE, VarMix, use_kh_struct, nkblock, kstart, kmax, &
24690 is, js, Ieq, Jeq, visc_limit_q_flag, sh_xy, str_xy, BS_coeff_q)
2470 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
2471 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure.
2472 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2473 type(MEKE_type), intent(in) :: MEKE !< MEKE fields related to Mesoscale Eddy Kinetic Energy.
2474 type(VarMix_CS), intent(in) :: VarMix !< Variable mixing control structure
2475 logical, intent(in) :: use_kh_struct !< If true, shape the backscatter coefficient
2476 !! with VarMix%BS_struct
2477 integer, intent(in) :: nkblock !< The k-block size used to size the following arrays [nondim]
2478 integer, intent(in) :: kstart !< The first absolute k-layer of the current k-block
2479 integer, intent(in) :: kmax !< The number of active k-layers in the current k-block
2480 integer, intent(in) :: is !< Start i-loop index for the q-point stress tensor
2481 integer, intent(in) :: js !< Start j-loop index for the q-point stress tensor
2482 integer, intent(in) :: Ieq !< End i-loop index for the q-point stress tensor
2483 integer, intent(in) :: Jeq !< End j-loop index for the q-point stress tensor
2484 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2485 intent(in) :: visc_limit_q_flag !< determines whether backscatter is shut off [nondim]
2486 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2487 intent(in) :: sh_xy !< horizontal shearing strain (du/dy + dv/dx) including
2488 !! metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
2489 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2490 intent(inout) :: str_xy !< The cross term in the stress tensor
2491 !! [H L2 T-2 ~> m3 s-2 or kg s-2]
2492 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2493 intent(inout) :: BS_coeff_q !< A diagnostic array of the backscatter
2494 !! coefficient [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2495 ! Local variables
2496 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock) :: &
24970 Kh_BS, & ! Laplacian antiviscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
24980 str_xy_BS ! The cross term in the stress tensor due to backscatter [H L2 T-2 ~> m3 s-2 or kg s-2]
2499 integer :: i, j, k, kk
2500
25010 do kk=1,kmax ; do J=js-1,Jeq ; do I=is-1,Ieq
25020 k = kstart + kk - 1
25030 if (visc_limit_q_flag(I,J,k) > 0) then
25040 Kh_BS(I,J,kk) = 0.
2505 else
25060 if (use_kh_struct) then
2507 Kh_BS(I,J,kk) = 0.25*( ((MEKE%Ku(i,j)*VarMix%BS_struct(i,j,k)) + &
2508 (MEKE%Ku(i+1,j+1)*VarMix%BS_struct(i+1,j+1,k))) + &
2509 ((MEKE%Ku(i+1,j)*VarMix%BS_struct(i+1,j,k)) + &
25100 (MEKE%Ku(i,j+1)*VarMix%BS_struct(i,j+1,k))) )
2511 else
2512 Kh_BS(I,J,kk) = 0.25*( (MEKE%Ku(i,j) + MEKE%Ku(i+1,j+1)) + &
25130 (MEKE%Ku(i+1,j) + MEKE%Ku(i,j+1)) )
2514 endif
2515 endif
2516 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
2517
25180 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
25190 str_xy_BS(I,J,kk) = -Kh_BS(I,J,kk) * (sh_xy(I,J,kk))
2520 enddo
2521
25220 if (CS%id_BS_coeff_q>0) then
25230 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
25240 k = kstart + kk - 1
25250 BS_coeff_q(I,J,k) = Kh_BS(I,J,kk)
2526 enddo
2527 endif
2528
25290 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, J=js-1:Jeq, I=is-1:Ieq)
25300 str_xy(I,J,kk) = str_xy(I,J,kk) + str_xy_BS(I,J,kk)
2531 enddo
2532
25330end subroutine hor_visc_backscatter_q
2534
2535!> Computes the Leith+E antisymmetric-viscosity ratio m_leithy, and updates the
2536!! biharmonic viscosity Ah (and Ah_h) to include the Leith+E contribution, optionally
2537!! smoothing m_leithy and Ah in the process. The caller must only invoke this
2538!! routine when CS%use_Leithy is true.
25390subroutine hor_visc_Leithy_Ah(G, GV, CS, nkblock, kstart, kmax, is_Kh, ie_Kh, &
25400 js_Kh, je_Kh, inv_PI6, Del2vort_q, vert_vort_mag, vert_vort_mag_smooth, &
25410 vort_xy_smooth, Ah, Ah_h, m_leithy, zc)
2542 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
2543 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure.
2544 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2545 integer, intent(in) :: nkblock !< The k-block size used to size the following arrays [nondim]
2546 integer, intent(in) :: kstart !< The first absolute k-layer of the current k-block
2547 integer, intent(in) :: kmax !< The number of active k-layers in the current k-block
2548 integer, intent(in) :: is_Kh !< Start i-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2549 integer, intent(in) :: ie_Kh !< End i-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2550 integer, intent(in) :: js_Kh !< Start j-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2551 integer, intent(in) :: je_Kh !< End j-loop index for the thickness point viscosities
2552 real, intent(in) :: inv_PI6 !< The inverse of pi to the sixth power [nondim]
2553 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2554 intent(in) :: Del2vort_q !< Laplacian of vorticity at q-points [L-2 T-1 ~> m-2 s-1]
2555 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2556 intent(in) :: vert_vort_mag !< Magnitude of the vertical vorticity
2557 !! gradient (h or q) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2558 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2559 intent(in) :: vert_vort_mag_smooth !< Magnitude of gradient of smoothed
2560 !! vertical vorticity (h or q) [L-1 T-1 ~> m-1 s-1]
2561 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2562 intent(in) :: vort_xy_smooth !< Vertical vorticity including metric
2563 !! terms, smoothed [T-1 ~> s-1]
2564 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2565 intent(inout) :: Ah !< biharmonic viscosity (h or q) [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1]
2566 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2567 intent(inout) :: Ah_h !< biharmonic viscosity at thickness points [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1]
2568 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2569 intent(out) :: m_leithy !< Kh=m_leithy*Ah in Leith+E parameterization [L-2 ~> m-2]
2570 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), intent(in), optional :: zc
2571 !< Depth at center of h cell [Z ~> m]
2572
2573 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock) :: &
25740 Ah_sq ! The square of the biharmonic viscosity [L8 T-2 ~> m8 s-2]
2575 real :: Del2vort_h ! Laplacian of vorticity at h-points [L-2 T-1 ~> m-2 s-1]
2576 real :: AhLth ! 2D Leith biharmonic viscosity [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1]
2577 real :: AhLthy ! 2D Leith+E biharmonic viscosity [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1]
2578 integer :: i, j, k, kk
2579
2580 !$omp target enter data map(alloc: Ah_sq)
2581
2582 ! Get m_leithy
25830 if (CS%smooth_Ah) then
25840 do kk=1,kmax
25850 m_leithy(:,:,kk) = 0.0 ! This is here to initialize domain edge halo values.
2586 enddo
2587 endif
2588
25890 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(Del2vort_h, AhLth))
2590 Del2vort_h = 0.25 * ((Del2vort_q(I,J,kk) + Del2vort_q(I-1,J-1,kk)) + &
25910 (Del2vort_q(I-1,J,kk) + Del2vort_q(I,J-1,kk)))
25920 AhLth = CS%Biharm6_const_xx(i,j) * inv_PI6 * abs(Del2vort_h)
25930 if (AhLth <= CS%Ah_bg_xx(i,j)) then
25940 m_leithy(i,j,kk) = 0.0
2595 else
25960 if ((CS%m_const_leithy(i,j)*vert_vort_mag(i,j,kk)) < abs(vort_xy_smooth(i,j,kk))) then
25970 m_leithy(i,j,kk) = CS%c_K * (vert_vort_mag(i,j,kk) / vort_xy_smooth(i,j,kk))**2
2598 else
25990 m_leithy(i,j,kk) = CS%m_leithy_max(i,j)
2600 endif
2601 endif
2602 enddo
2603
26040 if (CS%taper_leithy) then
2605 ! Multiply m_leithy by taper function of depth
26060 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
26070 k = kstart + kk - 1
26080 m_leithy(i,j,kk) = m_leithy(i,j,kk) * leithy_taper_function(CS, zc(i,j,k))
2609 enddo
2610 endif
2611
26120 if (CS%smooth_Ah) then
26130 do kk=1,kmax
2614 ! Smooth m_leithy. A single call smoothes twice.
2615 ! TODO: Port to GPU
2616 !$omp target update from(m_leithy)
26170 call pass_var(m_leithy(:,:,kk), G%Domain, halo=2)
26180 call smooth_x9_h(CS, G, m_leithy(:,:,kk), zero_land=.true.)
26190 call pass_var(m_leithy(:,:,kk), G%Domain)
2620 !$omp target update to(m_leithy)
2621 enddo
2622 endif
2623
2624 ! Get Ah
26250 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(Del2vort_h, AhLthy))
2626 Del2vort_h = 0.25 * ((Del2vort_q(I,J,kk) + Del2vort_q(I-1,J-1,kk)) + &
26270 (Del2vort_q(I-1,J,kk) + Del2vort_q(I,J-1,kk)))
2628 AhLthy = CS%Biharm6_const_xx(i,j) * inv_PI6 * &
26290 sqrt(max(0.,Del2vort_h**2 - m_leithy(i,j,kk)*vert_vort_mag_smooth(i,j,kk)**2))
26300 Ah(i,j,kk) = max(CS%Ah_bg_xx(i,j), AhLthy)
2631 enddo
26320 if (CS%smooth_Ah) then
2633 ! Smooth Ah before applying upper bound. Square Ah, then smooth, then take its square root.
26340 do kk=1,kmax
26350 Ah_sq(:,:,kk) = 0.0 ! This is here to initialize domain edge halo values.
2636 enddo
26370 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh)
26380 Ah_sq(i,j,kk) = Ah(i,j,kk)**2
2639 enddo
26400 do kk=1,kmax
2641 ! TODO: Port to GPU
2642 !$omp target update from(Ah_sq)
26430 call pass_var(Ah_sq(:,:,kk), G%Domain, halo=2)
2644 ! A single call smoothes twice.
26450 call smooth_x9_h(CS, G, Ah_sq(:,:,kk), zero_land=.false.)
26460 call pass_var(Ah_sq(:,:,kk), G%Domain)
2647 !$omp target update to(Ah_sq)
2648 enddo
26490 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
26500 k = kstart + kk - 1
26510 Ah_h(i,j,k) = max(CS%Ah_bg_xx(i,j), sqrt(max(0., Ah_sq(i,j,kk))))
26520 Ah(i,j,kk) = Ah_h(i,j,k)
2653 enddo
2654 else
26550 do concurrent (kk=1:kmax, j=js_Kh:je_Kh, i=is_Kh:ie_Kh) DO_LOCALITY(local(k))
26560 k = kstart + kk - 1
26570 Ah_h(i,j,k) = Ah(i,j,kk)
2658 enddo
2659 endif
2660
2661 !$omp target exit data map(delete: Ah_sq)
26620end subroutine hor_visc_Leithy_Ah
2663
2664!> Returns the effective k-block size for horizontal_viscosity calls.
26655integer function hor_visc_nkblock(CS, GV)
2666 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2667 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure.
26685 hor_visc_nkblock = merge(GV%ke, CS%nkblock, CS%nkblock==0)
26695end function hor_visc_nkblock
2670
2671!> Calculates the barotropic tension and shearing strain fields and the GME
2672!! efficiency and isopycnal height diffusivity fields that are used within
2673!! horizontal_viscosity when GME (CS%use_GME) is active. The caller must
2674!! only invoke this routine when CS%use_GME is true.
26750subroutine hor_visc_GME_setup(G, GV, US, CS, h, BT, TD, nkblock, &
26760 dudx_bt, dvdy_bt, dvdx_bt, dudy_bt, sh_xx_bt, sh_xy_bt, &
26770 GME_effic_h, GME_effic_q, KH_u_GME, KH_v_GME, &
26780 GME_coeff_h, GME_coeff_q, str_xx_GME, str_xy_GME)
2679 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
2680 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure.
2681 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
2682 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2683 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2684 intent(inout) :: h !< Layer thicknesses [H ~> m or kg m-2].
2685 type(barotropic_CS), optional, intent(in) :: BT !< Barotropic control structure
2686 type(thickness_diffuse_CS), optional, intent(in) :: TD !< Thickness diffusion control structure
2687 integer, intent(in) :: nkblock !< The k-block size used to size the following arrays [nondim]
2688 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)), &
2689 intent(out) :: dudx_bt !< x-component in the barotropic
2690 !! horizontal tension [T-1 ~> s-1]
2691 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)), &
2692 intent(out) :: dvdy_bt !< y-component in the barotropic
2693 !! horizontal tension [T-1 ~> s-1]
2694 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)), &
2695 intent(out) :: dvdx_bt !< x-component in the barotropic
2696 !! shearing strain [T-1 ~> s-1]
2697 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)), &
2698 intent(out) :: dudy_bt !< y-component in the barotropic
2699 !! shearing strain [T-1 ~> s-1]
2700 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), &
2701 intent(out) :: sh_xx_bt !< Barotropic horizontal tension
2702 !! (du/dx - dv/dy) including metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
2703 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)), &
2704 intent(out) :: sh_xy_bt !< Barotropic horizontal shearing strain
2705 !! (du/dy + dv/dx) including metric terms [T-1 ~> s-1]
2706 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), &
2707 intent(out) :: GME_effic_h !< The filtered efficiency of the
2708 !! GME terms at h points [nondim]
2709 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)), &
2710 intent(out) :: GME_effic_q !< The filtered efficiency of the
2711 !! GME terms at q points [nondim]
2712 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), &
2713 intent(out) :: KH_u_GME !< Isopycnal height diffusivities in
2714 !! u-columns [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2715 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)+1), &
2716 intent(out) :: KH_v_GME !< Isopycnal height diffusivities in
2717 !! v-columns [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2718 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),SZK_(G)), &
2719 intent(out) :: GME_coeff_h !< GME coefficient at h-points
2720 !! [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2721 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),SZK_(GV)), &
2722 intent(out) :: GME_coeff_q !< GME coeff. at q-points [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2723 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G),nkblock), &
2724 intent(out) :: str_xx_GME !< Smoothed diagonal term in the
2725 !! stress tensor from GME [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
2726 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G),nkblock), &
2727 intent(out) :: str_xy_GME !< Smoothed cross term in the
2728 !! stress tensor from GME [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
2729 ! Local variables
2730 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: &
27310 ubtav ! zonal barotropic velocity averaged over a baroclinic time-step [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
2732 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)) :: &
27330 vbtav ! meridional barotropic velocity averaged over a baroclinic time-step [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
2734 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: &
27350 htot ! The total thickness of all layers [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2736 real :: grad_vel_mag_bt_h ! Magnitude of the barotropic velocity gradient tensor squared at h-points [T-2 ~> s-2]
2737 real :: boundary_mask_h ! A mask that zeroes out cells with at least one land edge [nondim]
2738 real :: grad_vel_mag_bt_q ! Magnitude of the barotropic velocity gradient tensor squared at q-points [T-2 ~> s-2]
2739 real :: boundary_mask_q ! A mask that zeroes out cells with at least one land edge [nondim]
2740 real :: h_arith_q ! The arithmetic mean total thickness at q points [H ~> m or kg m-2]
2741 real :: I_GME_h0 ! The inverse of GME tapering scale [H-1 ~> m-1 or m2 kg-1]
2742 integer :: i, j, k, is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq, nz
2743
27440 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec ; nz = GV%ke
27450 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
2746
2747 ! Initialize diagnostic arrays with zeros
27480 GME_coeff_h(:,:,:) = 0.0
27490 GME_coeff_q(:,:,:) = 0.0
27500 str_xx_GME(:,:,:) = 0.0
27510 str_xy_GME(:,:,:) = 0.0
2752
2753 ! Get barotropic velocities and their gradients
27540 call barotropic_get_tav(BT, ubtav, vbtav, G, US)
2755
27560 call pass_vector(ubtav, vbtav, G%Domain)
27570 call pass_var(h, G%domain, halo=2)
2758
2759 ! Calculate the barotropic horizontal tension
27600 do j=js-2,je+2 ; do i=is-2,ie+2
2761 dudx_bt(i,j) = CS%DY_dxT(i,j)*((G%IdyCu(I,j) * ubtav(I,j)) - &
27620 (G%IdyCu(I-1,j) * ubtav(I-1,j)))
2763 dvdy_bt(i,j) = CS%DX_dyT(i,j)*((G%IdxCv(i,J) * vbtav(i,J)) - &
27640 (G%IdxCv(i,J-1) * vbtav(i,J-1)))
2765 enddo ; enddo
27660 do j=Jsq-1,Jeq+2 ; do i=Isq-1,Ieq+2
27670 sh_xx_bt(i,j) = dudx_bt(i,j) - dvdy_bt(i,j)
2768 enddo ; enddo
2769
2770 ! Components for the barotropic shearing strain
27710 do J=Jsq-2,Jeq+2 ; do I=Isq-2,Ieq+2
2772 dvdx_bt(I,J) = CS%DY_dxBu(I,J)*((vbtav(i+1,J)*G%IdyCv(i+1,J)) &
27730 - (vbtav(i,J)*G%IdyCv(i,J)))
2774 dudy_bt(I,J) = CS%DX_dyBu(I,J)*((ubtav(I,j+1)*G%IdxCu(I,j+1)) &
27750 - (ubtav(I,j)*G%IdxCu(I,j)))
2776 enddo ; enddo
2777
27780 if (CS%no_slip) then
27790 do J=js-2,je+1 ; do I=is-2,ie+1
27800 sh_xy_bt(I,J) = (2.0-G%mask2dBu(I,J)) * ( dvdx_bt(I,J) + dudy_bt(I,J) )
2781 enddo ; enddo
2782 else
27830 do J=js-2,je+1 ; do I=is-2,ie+1
27840 sh_xy_bt(I,J) = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * ( dvdx_bt(I,J) + dudy_bt(I,J) )
2785 enddo ; enddo
2786 endif
2787
27880 do j=js-2,je+2 ; do i=is-2,ie+2
27890 htot(i,j) = 0.0
2790 enddo ; enddo
27910 do k=1,nz ; do j=js-2,je+2 ; do i=is-2,ie+2
27920 htot(i,j) = htot(i,j) + h(i,j,k)
2793 enddo ; enddo ; enddo
2794
27950 I_GME_h0 = 1.0 / CS%GME_h0
27960 do j=Jsq-1,Jeq+2 ; do i=Isq-1,Ieq+2
27970 boundary_mask_h = (G%mask2dCu(I,j) * G%mask2dCu(I-1,j)) * (G%mask2dCv(i,J) * G%mask2dCv(i,J-1))
2798 grad_vel_mag_bt_h = G%mask2dT(I,J) * boundary_mask_h * (dudx_bt(i,j)**2 + dvdy_bt(i,j)**2 + &
2799 (0.25*((dvdx_bt(I,J)+dvdx_bt(I-1,J-1)) + (dvdx_bt(I,J-1)+dvdx_bt(I-1,J))))**2 + &
28000 (0.25*((dudy_bt(I,J)+dudy_bt(I-1,J-1)) + (dudy_bt(I,J-1)+dudy_bt(I-1,J))))**2)
2801 ! Probably the following test could be simplified to
2802 ! if (boundary_mask_h * G%mask2dT(I,J) > 0.0) then
28030 if (grad_vel_mag_bt_h > 0.0) then
28040 GME_effic_h(i,j) = CS%GME_efficiency * G%mask2dT(I,J) * (MIN(htot(i,j) * I_GME_h0, 1.0)**2)
2805 else
28060 GME_effic_h(i,j) = 0.0
2807 endif
2808 enddo ; enddo
2809
28100 do J=js-2,je+1 ; do I=is-2,ie+1
28110 boundary_mask_q = (G%mask2dCv(i,J) * G%mask2dCv(i+1,J)) * (G%mask2dCu(I,j) * G%mask2dCu(I,j+1))
2812 grad_vel_mag_bt_q = G%mask2dBu(I,J) * boundary_mask_q * (dvdx_bt(I,J)**2 + dudy_bt(I,J)**2 + &
2813 (0.25*((dudx_bt(i,j)+dudx_bt(i+1,j+1)) + (dudx_bt(i,j+1)+dudx_bt(i+1,j))))**2 + &
28140 (0.25*((dvdy_bt(i,j)+dvdy_bt(i+1,j+1)) + (dvdy_bt(i,j+1)+dvdy_bt(i+1,j))))**2)
2815 ! Probably the following test could be simplified to
2816 ! if (boundary_mask_q * G%mask2dBu(I,J) > 0.0) then
28170 if (grad_vel_mag_bt_q > 0.0) then
28180 h_arith_q = 0.25 * ((htot(i,j) + htot(i+1,j+1)) + (htot(i+1,j) + htot(i,j+1)))
28190 GME_effic_q(I,J) = CS%GME_efficiency * G%mask2dBu(I,J) * (MIN(h_arith_q * I_GME_h0, 1.0)**2)
2820 else
28210 GME_effic_q(I,J) = 0.0
2822 endif
2823 enddo ; enddo
2824
28250 call thickness_diffuse_get_KH(TD, KH_u_GME, KH_v_GME, G, GV)
2826
28270 call pass_vector(KH_u_GME, KH_v_GME, G%domain, To_All+Scalar_Pair)
2828
28290 if (CS%debug) &
28300 call uvchksum("GME KH[u,v]_GME", KH_u_GME, KH_v_GME, G%HI, haloshift=2, unscale=US%L_to_m**2*US%s_to_T)
2831
28320end subroutine hor_visc_GME_setup
2833
2834!> Allocates space for and calculates static variables used by horizontal_viscosity.
2835!! hor_visc_init calculates and stores the values of a number of metric functions that
2836!! are used in horizontal_viscosity.
28371subroutine hor_visc_init(Time, G, GV, US, param_file, diag, CS, ADp)
2838 type(time_type), intent(in) :: Time !< Current model time.
2839 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(inout) :: G !< The ocean's grid structure.
2840 type(verticalGrid_type), intent(in) :: GV !< The ocean's vertical grid structure
2841 type(unit_scale_type), intent(in) :: US !< A dimensional unit scaling type
2842 type(param_file_type), intent(in) :: param_file !< A structure to parse for run-time
2843 !! parameters.
2844 type(diag_ctrl), target, intent(inout) :: diag !< Structure to regulate diagnostic output.
2845 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Horizontal viscosity control structure
2846 type(accel_diag_ptrs), intent(in), optional :: ADp !< Acceleration diagnostics
2847
2848 ! u0v is the Laplacian sensitivities to the v velocities at u points, with u0u, v0u, and v0v defined analogously.
28493 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: u0u, u0v ! Laplacian sensitivities at u points [L-2 ~> m-2]
28501 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)) :: v0u, v0v ! Laplacian sensitivities at v points [L-2 ~> m-2]
2851 real :: grid_sp_h2 ! Harmonic mean of the squares of the grid [L2 ~> m2]
2852 real :: grid_sp_h3 ! Harmonic mean of the squares of the grid^(3/2) [L3 ~> m3]
2853 real :: grid_sp_q2 ! spacings at h and q points [L2 ~> m2]
2854 real :: grid_sp_q3 ! spacings at h and q points^(3/2) [L3 ~> m3]
2855 real :: min_grid_sp_h2 ! Minimum value of grid_sp_h2 [L2 ~> m2]
2856 real :: min_grid_sp_h4 ! Minimum value of grid_sp_h2**2 [L4 ~> m4]
2857 real :: Kh_Limit ! A coefficient [T-1 ~> s-1] used, along with the
2858 ! grid spacing, to limit Laplacian viscosity.
2859 real :: fmax ! maximum absolute value of f at the four
2860 ! vorticity points around a thickness point [T-1 ~> s-1]
2861 real :: BoundCorConst ! A constant used when using viscosity to bound the Coriolis accelerations
2862 ! [T2 L-2 ~> s2 m-2]
2863 real :: Ah_Limit ! coefficient [T-1 ~> s-1] used, along with the
2864 ! grid spacing, to limit biharmonic viscosity
2865 real :: Kh ! Lapacian horizontal viscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2866 real :: Ah ! biharmonic horizontal viscosity [L4 T-1 ~> m4 s-1]
2867 real :: Kh_vel_scale ! this speed [L T-1 ~> m s-1] times grid spacing gives Laplacian viscosity
2868 real :: Ah_vel_scale ! this speed [L T-1 ~> m s-1] times grid spacing cubed gives biharmonic viscosity
2869 real :: Ah_time_scale ! damping time-scale for biharmonic visc [T ~> s]
2870 real :: Smag_Lap_const ! nondimensional Laplacian Smagorinsky constant [nondim]
2871 real :: Smag_bi_const ! nondimensional biharmonic Smagorinsky constant [nondim]
2872 real :: Leith_Lap_const ! nondimensional Laplacian Leith constant [nondim]
2873 real :: Leith_bi_const ! nondimensional biharmonic Leith constant [nondim]
2874 real :: dt ! The dynamics time step [T ~> s]
2875 real :: Idt ! The inverse of dt [T-1 ~> s-1]
2876 real :: denom ! work variable; the denominator of a fraction [L-2 ~> m-2] or [L-4 ~> m-4]
2877 real :: maxvel ! largest permitted velocity components [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
2878 real :: bound_Cor_vel ! grid-scale velocity variations at which value
2879 ! the quadratically varying biharmonic viscosity
2880 ! balances Coriolis acceleration [L T-1 ~> m s-1]
2881 real :: Kh_sin_lat ! Amplitude of latitudinally dependent viscosity [L2 T-1 ~> m2 s-1]
2882 real :: Kh_pwr_of_sine ! Power used to raise sin(lat) when using Kh_sin_lat [nondim]
2883 logical :: bound_Cor_def ! parameter setting of BOUND_CORIOLIS
2884 logical :: split ! If true, use the split time stepping scheme.
2885 ! If false and USE_GME = True, issue a FATAL error.
2886 logical :: use_MEKE ! If true, the MEKE parameterization is in use.
2887 logical :: enable_bugs ! If true, the defaults for recently added bug-fix flags are set to
2888 ! recreate the bugs, or if false bugs are only used if actively selected.
2889 real :: backscatter_Ro_c ! Coefficient in Rossby number function for backscatter [nondim]
2890 integer :: default_answer_date ! The default setting for the various ANSWER_DATE flags
2891 character(len=200) :: inputdir, filename ! Input file names and paths
2892 character(len=80) :: Kh_var ! Input variable names
2893 real :: deg2rad ! Converts degrees to radians [radians degree-1]
2894 real :: slat_fn ! sin(lat)**Kh_pwr_of_sine [nondim]
2895 real :: aniso_grid_dir(2) ! Vector (n1,n2) for anisotropic direction [nondim]
2896 integer :: aniso_mode ! Selects the mode for setting the anisotropic direction
2897 integer :: is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq, nz
2898 integer :: isd, ied, jsd, jed, IsdB, IedB, JsdB, JedB
2899 integer :: i, j
2900#ifdef __NVCOMPILER_OPENMP_GPU
2901 integer, parameter :: default_nkblock = 0
2902#else
2903 integer, parameter :: default_nkblock = 1
2904#endif
2905 ! This include declares and sets the variable "version".
2906# include "version_variable.h"
2907 character(len=40) :: mdl = "MOM_hor_visc" ! module name
29081 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec ; nz = GV%ke
29091 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
29101 isd = G%isd ; ied = G%ied ; jsd = G%jsd ; jed = G%jed
29111 IsdB = G%IsdB ; IedB = G%IedB ; JsdB = G%JsdB ; JedB = G%JedB
2912
2913 ! init control structure
29141 call ZB2020_init(Time, G, GV, US, param_file, diag, CS%ZB2020, CS%use_ZB2020)
2915
29161 CS%initialized = .true.
2917
29181 CS%diag => diag
2919 ! Read parameters and write them to the model log.
29201 call log_version(param_file, mdl, version, "")
2921
2922 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "HORVISC_NKBLOCK", CS%nkblock, &
2923 "The k-direction block size used in horizontal viscosity calculations. "//&
2924 "The default 0 setting dynamically uses the full vertical column.", &
29251 default=default_nkblock, layoutParam=.true.)
29261 if (CS%nkblock < 0) call MOM_error(FATAL, "HORVISC_NKBLOCK must be >= 0.")
2927
2928 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "USE_CIRCULATION_IN_HORVISC", CS%use_circulation, &
2929 "Use circulation theorem to compute vorticity in horvisc module (for ZB20 or Leith)", &
29301 default=.False.)
2931

2934 "This sets the default value for the various _ANSWER_DATE parameters.", &
29351 default=99991231)

2938 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "USE_MEKE", use_MEKE, &
29391 default=.false., do_not_log=.true.)
29401 backscatter_Ro_c = 0.0
29411 if (use_MEKE) call get_param(param_file, mdl, "MEKE_BACKSCAT_RO_C", backscatter_Ro_c, &
2942 "The coefficient in the Rossby number function for scaling the biharmonic "//&
2943 "frictional energy source. Setting to non-zero enables the Rossby number function.", &
29440 units="nondim", default=0.0, do_not_log=.true.)
2945
2946 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "HOR_VISC_ANSWER_DATE", CS%answer_date, &
2947 "The vintage of the order of arithmetic and expressions in the horizontal "//&
2948 "viscosity calculations. Values between 20190102 and 20241201 recover the "//&
2949 "answers from the end of 2018, while higher values use updated and more robust "//&
2950 "forms of the same expressions.", &
29511 default=default_answer_date, do_not_log=(.not.GV%Boussinesq).or.(backscatter_Ro_c==0.0))
29521 if (.not.GV%Boussinesq) CS%answer_date = max(CS%answer_date, 20241201)
2953
29541 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "DEBUG", CS%debug, default=.false.)

29571 "currently only works with split mode.", default=.false.)

2960 "USE_CONT_THICKNESS=True. This is not recommended.", &
29611 default=.false., do_not_log=.not.CS%use_cont_thick)

2964 "If true, use a Laplacian horizontal viscosity.", &
29651 default=.false.)

29701 do_not_log=.not.CS%Laplacian)

2972 "The minimum value allowed for Laplacian horizontal viscosity, KH.", &

30021 default=.false., do_not_log=.not.CS%Laplacian)
30031 if (.not.CS%Laplacian) CS%Leith_Kh = .false.

30121 if (.not.(CS%Laplacian.and.use_MEKE)) CS%res_scale_MEKE = .false.
3013

30161 "to be stable.", default=.true., do_not_log=.not.CS%Laplacian)

3023 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "ANISOTROPIC_VISCOSITY", CS%anisotropic, &

30261 do_not_log=.not.CS%Laplacian)
30271 if (.not.CS%Laplacian) CS%anisotropic = .false. ! This replicates the prior code, but is it intended?

3034 "The background Laplacian anisotropic horizontal viscosity.", &

30361 do_not_log=.not.CS%anisotropic)

3040 "\t 1 - Points towards East.\n"//&
3041 "\t 2 - Points along the flow direction, U/|U|.", &
30421 default=0, do_not_log=.not.CS%anisotropic)
30431 if (aniso_mode == 0) then
3044 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "ANISO_GRID_DIR", aniso_grid_dir, &
3045 "The vector pointing in the direction of anisotropy for horizontal viscosity. "//&
3046 "n1,n2 are the i,j components relative to the grid.", &
30471 units="nondim", fail_if_missing=CS%anisotropic, do_not_log=.not.CS%anisotropic)
30480 elseif (aniso_mode == 1) then
3049 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "ANISO_GRID_DIR", aniso_grid_dir, &
3050 "The vector pointing in the direction of anisotropy for horizontal viscosity. "//&
3051 "n1,n2 are the i,j components relative to the spherical coordinates.", &
30520 units="nondim", fail_if_missing=CS%anisotropic, do_not_log=.not.CS%anisotropic)
3053 else
3054 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "ANISO_GRID_DIR", aniso_grid_dir, &
3055 "The vector pointing in the direction of anisotropy for horizontal viscosity.", &
30560 units="nondim", fail_if_missing=.false., do_not_log=.true.)
3057 endif
3058
3059 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "BIHARMONIC", CS%biharmonic, &
3060 "If true, use a biharmonic horizontal viscosity. "//&
3061 "BIHARMONIC may be used with LAPLACIAN.", &
30621 default=.true.)
3063 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "AH", Ah, &
3064 "The background biharmonic horizontal viscosity.", &
3065 units="m4 s-1", default=0.0, scale=US%m_to_L**4*US%T_to_s, &
30661 do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
3067 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "AH_VEL_SCALE", Ah_vel_scale, &
3068 "The velocity scale which is multiplied by the cube of "//&
3069 "the grid spacing to calculate the biharmonic viscosity. "//&
3070 "The final viscosity is the largest of this scaled "//&
3071 "viscosity, the Smagorinsky and Leith viscosities, and AH.", &
30721 units="m s-1", default=0.0, scale=US%m_s_to_L_T, do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
3073 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "AH_TIME_SCALE", Ah_time_scale, &
3074 "A time scale whose inverse is multiplied by the fourth "//&
3075 "power of the grid spacing to calculate biharmonic viscosity. "//&
3076 "The final viscosity is the largest of all viscosity "//&
3077 "formulations in use. 0.0 means that it's not used.", &
30781 units="s", default=0.0, scale=US%s_to_T, do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
3079 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "SMAGORINSKY_AH", CS%Smagorinsky_Ah, &
3080 "If true, use a biharmonic Smagorinsky nonlinear eddy "//&
30811 "viscosity.", default=.false., do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
30821 if (.not.CS%biharmonic) CS%Smagorinsky_Ah = .false.
3083 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "LEITH_AH", CS%Leith_Ah, &
3084 "If true, use a biharmonic Leith nonlinear eddy "//&
30851 "viscosity.", default=.false., do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
30861 if (.not.CS%biharmonic) CS%Leith_Ah = .false.
3087 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "USE_LEITHY", CS%use_Leithy, &
3088 "If true, use a biharmonic Leith nonlinear eddy "//&
3089 "viscosity together with a harmonic backscatter.", &
30901 default=.false.)
30911 if (CS%EY24_EBT_BS .and. CS%use_Leithy) then
3092 call MOM_error(FATAL, "MOM_hor_visc.F90, hor_visc_init:"//&
30930 "Cannot simultaneously use EY24 EBT backscatter and Leith+E backscatter")
3094 endif
3095 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "BOUND_AH", CS%bound_Ah, &
3096 "If true, the biharmonic coefficient is locally limited "//&
30971 "to be stable.", default=.true., do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
30981 if (.not.CS%biharmonic) CS%bound_Ah = .false.
3099 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "RE_AH", CS%Re_Ah, &
3100 "If nonzero, the biharmonic coefficient is scaled "//&
3101 "so that the biharmonic Reynolds number is equal to this.", &
31021 units="nondim", default=0.0, do_not_log=.not.CS%biharmonic)
3103
3104 call get_param(param_file, mdl, "BACKSCATTER_UNDERBOUND", CS%backscatter_underbound, &
3105 "If true, the bounds on the biharmonic viscosity are allowed to "//&
3106 "increase where the Laplacian viscosity is negative (due to backscatter "//&
3107 "parameterizations) beyond the largest timestep-dependent stable values of "//&
3108 "biharmonic viscosity when no Laplacian viscosity is applied. The default "//&
3109 "is true for historical reasons, but this option probably should not be used "//&
3110 "because it can contribute to numerical instabilities.", &

34520 grid_sp_h2 = (2.0*CS%dx2h(i,j)*CS%dy2h(i,j)) / (CS%dx2h(i,j) + CS%dy2h(i,j))

3465 endif

3882!> leithy_taper_function returns 1 if zc is shallower than leithy_depth; 0 if deeper than
3883!! leithy_depth+leithy_width; and an interpolating cubic spline in between.
38840pure function leithy_taper_function(CS, zc)
3885 !$omp declare target
3886 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure for horizontal viscosity
3887 real, intent(in) :: zc !< depth of h-cell centersi [Z ~> m]
3888 real :: leithy_taper_function ! Taper function evaluated at zc [nondim]
3889
3890 ! Local variables
3891 real :: x ! 0 at top of transition and 1 at bottom [nondim]
3892
38930 x = (zc - CS%leithy_depth) / CS%leithy_width
38940 if (zc <= CS%leithy_depth) then
38950 leithy_taper_function = 1.0
38960 elseif (zc >= CS%leithy_depth + CS%leithy_width) then
38970 leithy_taper_function = 0.0
3898 else
38990 leithy_taper_function = (x - 1.0)**2 * (1.0 + 2 * x)
3900 endif
39010end function leithy_taper_function

3903!> hor_visc_vel_stencil returns the horizontal viscosity input velocity stencil size
39044function hor_visc_vel_stencil(CS) result(stencil)
3905 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure for horizontal viscosity
3906 integer :: stencil !< The horizontal viscosity velocity stencil size with the current settings.
3907
39084 stencil = 2
3909
39104 if ((CS%Leith_Kh) .or. (CS%Leith_Ah) .or. (CS%use_Leithy)) then
39110 stencil = 3
3912 endif
39134end function hor_visc_vel_stencil
3914
3915!> Calculates factors in the anisotropic orientation tensor to be align with the grid.
3916!! With n1=1 and n2=0, this recovers the approach of Large et al, 2001.
39170subroutine align_aniso_tensor_to_grid(CS, n1, n2)
3918 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(inout) :: CS !< Control structure for horizontal viscosity
3919 real, intent(in) :: n1 !< i-component of direction vector [nondim]
3920 real, intent(in) :: n2 !< j-component of direction vector [nondim]
3921 ! Local variables
3922 real :: recip_n2_norm ! The inverse of the squared magnitude of n1 and n2 [nondim]
3923 ! For normalizing n=(n1,n2) in case arguments are not a unit vector
39240 recip_n2_norm = (n1**2) + (n2**2)
39250 if (recip_n2_norm > 0.) recip_n2_norm = 1. / recip_n2_norm
39260 CS%n1n2_h(:,:) = 2. * ( n1 * n2 ) * recip_n2_norm
39270 CS%n1n2_q(:,:) = 2. * ( n1 * n2 ) * recip_n2_norm
39280 CS%n1n1_m_n2n2_h(:,:) = ( (n1 * n1) - (n2 * n2) ) * recip_n2_norm
39290 CS%n1n1_m_n2n2_q(:,:) = ( (n1 * n1) - (n2 * n2) ) * recip_n2_norm
39300end subroutine align_aniso_tensor_to_grid
3931
3932!> Apply a 1-1-4-1-1 Laplacian filter one time on GME diffusive flux to reduce any
3933!! horizontal two-grid-point noise
39340subroutine smooth_GME(CS, G, GME_flux_h, GME_flux_q)
3935 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure
3936 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid
3937 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), optional, intent(inout) :: GME_flux_h!< GME diffusive flux
3938 !! at h points [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
3939 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)), optional, intent(inout) :: GME_flux_q!< GME diffusive flux
3940 !! at q points [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
3941 ! local variables
39420 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)) :: GME_flux_h_original ! The previous value of GME_flux_h [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
39430 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJB_(G)) :: GME_flux_q_original ! The previous value of GME_flux_q [L2 T-2 ~> m2 s-2]
3944 real :: wc, ww, we, wn, ws ! averaging weights for smoothing [nondim]
3945 integer :: i, j, s, halosz
3946 integer :: xh, xq ! The number of valid extra halo points for h and q points.
3947 integer :: is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq
3948
39490 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
39500 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
39510 xh = 0 ; xq = 0
3952
39530 do s=1,CS%num_smooth_gme
39540 if (present(GME_flux_h)) then
39550 if (xh < 0) then
3956 ! Update halos if needed, but avoid doing so more often than is needed.
39570 halosz = min(G%isc-G%isd, G%jsc-G%jsd, 2+CS%num_smooth_gme-s)
39580 call pass_var(GME_flux_h, G%Domain, halo=halosz)
39590 xh = halosz - 2
3960 endif
39610 GME_flux_h_original(:,:) = GME_flux_h(:,:)
3962 ! apply smoothing on GME
39630 do j=Jsq-xh,Jeq+1+xh ; do i=Isq-xh,Ieq+1+xh
3964 ! skip land points
39650 if (G%mask2dT(i,j)==0.) cycle
3966 ! compute weights
39670 ww = 0.125 * G%mask2dT(i-1,j)
39680 we = 0.125 * G%mask2dT(i+1,j)
39690 ws = 0.125 * G%mask2dT(i,j-1)
39700 wn = 0.125 * G%mask2dT(i,j+1)
39710 wc = 1.0 - ((ww+we)+(wn+ws))
3972 GME_flux_h(i,j) = wc * GME_flux_h_original(i,j) &
3973 + ((ww * GME_flux_h_original(i-1,j) + we * GME_flux_h_original(i+1,j)) &
39740 + (ws * GME_flux_h_original(i,j-1) + wn * GME_flux_h_original(i,j+1)))
3975 enddo ; enddo
39760 xh = xh - 1
3977 endif
39780 if (present(GME_flux_q)) then
39790 if (xq < 0) then
3980 ! Update halos if needed, but avoid doing so more often than is needed.
39810 halosz = min(G%isc-G%isd, G%jsc-G%jsd, 2+CS%num_smooth_gme-s)
39820 call pass_var(GME_flux_q, G%Domain, position=CORNER, complete=.true., halo=halosz)
39830 xq = halosz - 2
3984 endif
39850 GME_flux_q_original(:,:) = GME_flux_q(:,:)
3986 ! apply smoothing on GME
39870 do J=js-1-xq,je+xq ; do I=is-1-xq,ie+xq
3988 ! skip land points
39890 if (G%mask2dBu(I,J)==0.) cycle
3990 ! compute weights
39910 ww = 0.125 * G%mask2dBu(I-1,J)
39920 we = 0.125 * G%mask2dBu(I+1,J)
39930 ws = 0.125 * G%mask2dBu(I,J-1)
39940 wn = 0.125 * G%mask2dBu(I,J+1)
39950 wc = 1.0 - ((ww+we)+(wn+ws))
3996 GME_flux_q(I,J) = wc * GME_flux_q_original(I,J) &
3997 + ((ww * GME_flux_q_original(I-1,J) + we * GME_flux_q_original(I+1,J)) &
39980 + (ws * GME_flux_q_original(I,J-1) + wn * GME_flux_q_original(I,J+1)))
3999 enddo ; enddo
40000 xq = xq - 1
4001 endif
4002 enddo ! s-loop
40030end subroutine smooth_GME
4004
4005!> Apply a 5x5 weighted sum. In exact arithmetic this is the same as applying a 1:2:1 smoother
4006!! twice in each direction. The implementation here uses fewer arithmetic operations, and is
4007!! rotationally symmetric. To obtain the weighted average, divide the result by 256.
40080function sum_5x5(x) result(sum_x)
4009 implicit none
4010 real, intent(in) :: x(:,:) !< 5x5 array to be summed. Assumed-shape to avoid copies. [arbitrary]
4011 real :: sum_x !< output [same as x]
4012 real :: sum_partial !< scalar holding a partial sum, for convenience
4013
40140 sum_partial = ((x(1,1) + x(5,5)) + (x(1,5) + x(5,1)))
40150 sum_partial = sum_partial + 6.*((x(3,1) + x(1,3)) + (x(3,5) + x(5,3)))
40160 sum_partial = sum_partial + 36.*x(3,3)
40170 sum_partial = sum_partial + 16.*((x(2,2) + x(4,4)) + (x(2,4) + x(4,2)))
4018 sum_x = 4.*( ((x(1,2) + x(2,1)) + (x(1,4) + x(4,1))) + &
40190 ((x(5,2) + x(2,5)) + (x(5,4) + x(4,5))) )
40200 sum_x = sum_x + 24.*((x(2,3) + x(3,2)) + (x(4,3) + x(3,4)))
40210 sum_x = sum_x + sum_partial
4022
40230end function
4024
4025!> Apply a 9-point smoothing filter twice to a field staggered at a thickness point to reduce
4026!! horizontal two-grid-point noise. Implemented using a single 5x5 pass rather than 3x3 twice.
4027!! Note that this subroutine does not conserve mass, so don't use it in situations where you
4028!! need conservation. Also note that it assumes that the input field has valid values in the
4029!! first two halo points upon entry.
40300subroutine smooth_x9_h(CS, G, field_h, zero_land)
4031 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure
4032 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid
4033 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(inout) :: field_h !< h-point field to be smoothed [arbitrary]
4034 logical, optional, intent(in) :: zero_land !< If present and false, return the average
4035 !! of the surrounding ocean points when
4036 !! smoothing, otherwise use a value of 0 for
4037 !! land points and include them in the averages.
4038
4039 ! Local variables
40400 real :: fh_prev(SZI_(G),SZJ_(G)) ! Copy of the input value of the h-point field [arbitrary]
4041 real :: Iwts_zl = 0.00390625 ! The inverse of the sum of the weights zeroing land, = 1/256 [nondim]
4042 logical :: zero_land_val ! The value of the zero_land optional argument or .true. if it is absent.
4043 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je
4044
40450 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
4046
40470 zero_land_val = .true. ; if (present(zero_land)) zero_land_val = zero_land
4048
40490 fh_prev(:,:) = field_h(:,:)
4050 ! apply smoothing on field_h using rotationally symmetric expressions.
40510 if (zero_land_val) then
40520 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie ; if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.0) then
40530 field_h(i,j) = Iwts_zl * sum_5x5(fh_prev(i-2:i+2,j-2:j+2) * G%mask2dT(i-2:i+2,j-2:j+2))
4054 endif ; enddo ; enddo
4055 else
40560 do j=js,je ; do i=is,ie ; if (G%mask2dT(i,j) > 0.0) then
40570 field_h(i,j) = CS%Iwts(i,j) * sum_5x5(fh_prev(i-2:i+2,j-2:j+2) * G%mask2dT(i-2:i+2,j-2:j+2))
4058 endif ; enddo ; enddo
4059 endif
4060
40610end subroutine smooth_x9_h
4062
4063!> Apply a 9-point smoothing filter twice to a pair of velocity components to reduce
4064!! horizontal two-grid-point noise. Implemented using a single 5x5 pass rather than 3x3 twice.
4065!! Note that this subroutine does not conserve angular momentum, so don't use it
4066!! in situations where you need conservation. Also note that it assumes that the
4067!! input fields have valid values in the first two halo points upon entry.
40680subroutine smooth_x9_uv(CS, G, field_u, field_v, zero_land)
4069 type(hor_visc_CS), intent(in) :: CS !< Control structure
4070 type(ocean_grid_type), intent(in) :: G !< Ocean grid
4071 real, dimension(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G)), intent(inout) :: field_u !< u-point field to be smoothed [arbitrary]
4072 real, dimension(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)), intent(inout) :: field_v !< v-point field to be smoothed [arbitrary]
4073 logical, optional, intent(in) :: zero_land !< If present and false, return the average
4074 !! of the surrounding ocean points when
4075 !! smoothing, otherwise use a value of 0 for
4076 !! land points and include them in the averages.
4077
4078 ! Local variables.
40790 real :: fu_prev(SZIB_(G),SZJ_(G)) ! Copy of the input value of the u-point field [arbitrary]
40800 real :: fv_prev(SZI_(G),SZJB_(G)) ! Copy of the input value of the v-point field [arbitrary]
4081 real :: Iwts_zl = 0.00390625 ! The inverse of the sum of the weights zeroing land, = 1/256 [nondim]
4082 logical :: zero_land_val ! The value of the zero_land optional argument or .true. if it is absent.
4083 integer :: i, j, is, ie, js, je, Isq, Ieq, Jsq, Jeq
4084
40850 is = G%isc ; ie = G%iec ; js = G%jsc ; je = G%jec
40860 Isq = G%IscB ; Ieq = G%IecB ; Jsq = G%JscB ; Jeq = G%JecB
4087
40880 zero_land_val = .true. ; if (present(zero_land)) zero_land_val = zero_land
4089
40900 fu_prev(:,:) = field_u(:,:)
4091 ! apply smoothing on field_u using the original non-rotationally symmetric expressions.
40920 if (zero_land_val) then
40930 do j=js,je ; do I=Isq,Ieq ; if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0) then
40940 field_u(I,j) = Iwts_zl * sum_5x5(fu_prev(I-2:I+2,j-2:j+2) * G%mask2dCu(I-2:I+2,j-2:j+2))
4095 endif ; enddo ; enddo
4096 else
40970 do j=js,je ; do I=Isq,Ieq ; if (G%mask2dCu(I,j) > 0.0) then
4098 field_u(I,j) = CS%Iwts_u(I,j) * &
40990 sum_5x5(fu_prev(I-2:I+2,j-2:j+2) * G%mask2dCu(I-2:I+2,j-2:j+2))
4100 endif ; enddo ; enddo
4101 endif
4102
41030 fv_prev(:,:) = field_v(:,:)
4104 ! apply smoothing on field_v using the original non-rotationally symmetric expressions.
41050 if (zero_land_val) then
41060 do J=Jsq,Jeq ; do i=is,ie ; if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0) then
41070 field_v(i,J) = Iwts_zl * sum_5x5(fv_prev(i-2:i+2,J-2:J+2) * G%mask2dCv(i-2:i+2,J-2:J+2))
4108 endif ; enddo ; enddo
4109 else
41100 do J=Jsq,Jeq ; do i=is,ie ; if (G%mask2dCv(i,J) > 0.0) then
4111 field_v(i,J) = CS%Iwts_v(i,J) * &
41120 sum_5x5(fv_prev(i-2:i+2,J-2:J+2) * G%mask2dCv(i-2:i+2,J-2:J+2))
4113 endif ; enddo ; enddo
4114 endif
4115
41160end subroutine smooth_x9_uv

4206!> \namespace mom_hor_visc
4207!!
4208!! \section section_horizontal_viscosity Horizontal viscosity in MOM
4209!!
4210!! This module contains the subroutine horizontal_viscosity that calculates the
4211!! effects of horizontal viscosity, including parameterizations of the value of
4212!! the viscosity itself. Subroutine horizontal_viscosity calculates the acceleration due to
4213!! some combination of a biharmonic viscosity and a Laplacian viscosity. Either or
4214!! both may use a coefficient that depends on the shear and strain of the flow.
4215!! All metric terms are retained. The Laplacian is calculated as the divergence of
4216!! a stress tensor, using the form suggested by \cite Smagorinsky1993. The biharmonic
4217!! is calculated by twice applying the divergence of the stress tensor that is
4218!! used to calculate the Laplacian, but without the dependence on thickness in the
4219!! first pass. This form permits a variable viscosity, and indicates no
4220!! acceleration for either resting fluid or solid body rotation.
4221!!
4222!! The form of the viscous accelerations is discussed extensively in \cite griffies2000,
4223!! and the implementation here follows that discussion closely.
4224!! We use the notation of \cite Smith2003 with the exception that the
4225!! isotropic viscosity is \f$\kappa_h\f$.
4226!!
4227!! In general, the horizontal stress tensor can be written as
4228!! \f[
4229!! {\bf \sigma} =
4230!! \begin{pmatrix}
4231!! \frac{1}{2} \left( \sigma_D + \sigma_T \right) & \frac{1}{2} \sigma_S \\\\
4232!! \frac{1}{2} \sigma_S & \frac{1}{2} \left( \sigma_D - \sigma_T \right)
4233!! \end{pmatrix}
4234!! \f]
4235!! where \f$\sigma_D\f$, \f$\sigma_T\f$ and \f$\sigma_S\f$ are stresses associated with
4236!! invariant factors in the strain-rate tensor. For a Newtonian fluid, the stress
4237!! tensor is usually linearly related to the strain-rate tensor. The horizontal
4238!! strain-rate tensor is
4239!! \f[
4240!! \dot{\bf e} =
4241!! \begin{pmatrix}
4242!! \frac{1}{2} \left( \dot{e}_D + \dot{e}_T \right) & \frac{1}{2} \dot{e}_S \\\\
4243!! \frac{1}{2} \dot{e}_S & \frac{1}{2} \left( \dot{e}_D - \dot{e}_T \right)
4244!! \end{pmatrix}
4245!! \f]
4246!! where \f$\dot{e}_D = \partial_x u + \partial_y v\f$ is the horizontal divergence,
4247!! \f$\dot{e}_T = \partial_x u - \partial_y v\f$ is the horizontal tension, and
4248!! \f$\dot{e}_S = \partial_y u + \partial_x v\f$ is the horizontal shear strain.
4249!!
4250!! The trace of the stress tensor, \f$tr(\bf \sigma) = \sigma_D\f$, is usually
4251!! absorbed into the pressure and only the deviatoric stress tensor considered.
4252!! From here on, we drop \f$\sigma_D\f$. The trace of the strain tensor, \f$tr(\bf e) =
4253!! \dot{e}_D\f$ is non-zero for horizontally divergent flow but only enters the
4254!! stress tensor through \f$\sigma_D\f$ and so we will drop \f$\sigma_D\f$ from
4255!! calculations of the strain tensor in the code. Therefore the horizontal stress
4256!! tensor can be considered to be
4257!! \f[
4258!! {\bf \sigma} =
4259!! \begin{pmatrix}
4260!! \frac{1}{2} \sigma_T & \frac{1}{2} \sigma_S \\\\
4261!! \frac{1}{2} \sigma_S & - \frac{1}{2} \sigma_T
4262!! \end{pmatrix}
4263!! .\f]
4264!!
4265!! The stresses above are linearly related to the strain through a viscosity
4266!! coefficient, \f$\kappa_h\f$:
4267!! \f{eqnarray*}{
4268!! \sigma_T & = & 2 \kappa_h \dot{e}_T \\\\
4269!! \sigma_S & = & 2 \kappa_h \dot{e}_S
4270!! .
4271!! \f}
4272!!
4273!! The viscosity \f$\kappa_h\f$ may either be a constant or variable. For example,
4274!! \f$\kappa_h\f$ may vary with the shear, as proposed by \cite Smagorinsky1993.
4275!!
4276!! The accelerations resulting form the divergence of the stress tensor are
4277!! \f{eqnarray*}{
4278!! \hat{\bf x} \cdot \left( \nabla \cdot {\bf \sigma} \right)
4279!! & = &
4280!! \partial_x \left( \frac{1}{2} \sigma_T \right)
4281!! + \partial_y \left( \frac{1}{2} \sigma_S \right)
4282!! \\\\
4283!! & = &
4284!! \partial_x \left( \kappa_h \dot{e}_T \right)
4285!! + \partial_y \left( \kappa_h \dot{e}_S \right)
4286!! \\\\
4287!! \hat{\bf y} \cdot \left( \nabla \cdot {\bf \sigma} \right)
4288!! & = &
4289!! \partial_x \left( \frac{1}{2} \sigma_S \right)
4290!! + \partial_y \left( - \frac{1}{2} \sigma_T \right)
4291!! \\\\
4292!! & = &
4293!! \partial_x \left( \kappa_h \dot{e}_S \right)
4294!! + \partial_y \left( - \kappa_h \dot{e}_T \right)
4295!! .
4296!! \f}
4297!!
4298!! The form of the Laplacian viscosity in general coordinates is:
4299!! \f{eqnarray*}{
4300!! \hat{\bf x} \cdot \left( \nabla \cdot \sigma \right)
4301!! & = &
4302!! \frac{1}{h} \left[ \partial_x \left( \kappa_h h \dot{e}_T \right)
4303!! + \partial_y \left( \kappa_h h \dot{e}_S \right) \right]
4304!! \\\\
4305!! \hat{\bf y} \cdot \left( \nabla \cdot \sigma \right)
4306!! & = &
4307!! \frac{1}{h} \left[ \partial_x \left( \kappa_h h \dot{e}_S \right)
4308!! - \partial_y \left( \kappa_h h \dot{e}_T \right) \right]
4309!! .
4310!! \f}
4311!!
4312!! \subsection section_laplacian_viscosity_coefficient Laplacian viscosity coefficient
4313!!
4314!! The horizontal viscosity coefficient, \f$\kappa_h\f$, can have multiple components.
4315!! The isotropic components are:
4316!! - A uniform background component, \f$\kappa_{bg}\f$.
4317!! - A constant but spatially variable 2D map, \f$\kappa_{2d}(x,y)\f$.
4318!! - A ''MICOM'' viscosity, \f$U_\nu \Delta(x,y)\f$, which uses a constant
4319!! velocity scale, \f$U_\nu\f$ and a measure of the grid-spacing \f$\Delta(x,y)^2 =
4320!! \frac{2 \Delta x^2 \Delta y^2}{\Delta x^2 + \Delta y^2}\f$.
4321!! - A function of
4322!! latitude, \f$\kappa_{\phi}(x,y) = \kappa_{\pi/2} |\sin(\phi)|^n\f$.
4323!! - A dynamic Smagorinsky viscosity, \f$\kappa_{Sm}(x,y,t) = C_{Sm} \Delta^2 \sqrt{\dot{e}_T^2 + \dot{e}_S^2}\f$.
4324!! - A dynamic Leith viscosity, \f$\kappa_{Lth}(x,y,t) =
4325!! C_{Lth} \Delta^3 \sqrt{|\nabla \zeta|^2 + |\nabla \dot{e}_D|^2}\f$.
4326!!
4327!! A maximum stable viscosity, \f$\kappa_{max}(x,y)\f$ is calculated based on the
4328!! grid-spacing and time-step and used to clip calculated viscosities.
4329!!
4330!! The static components of \f$\kappa_h\f$ are first combined as follows:
4331!! \f[
4332!! \kappa_{static} = \min \left[ \max\left(
4333!! \kappa_{bg},
4334!! U_\nu \Delta(x,y),
4335!! \kappa_{2d}(x,y),
4336!! \kappa_\phi(x,y)
4337!! \right)
4338!! , \kappa_{max}(x,y) \right]
4339!! \f]
4340!! and stored in the module control structure as variables <code>Kh_bg_xx</code> and
4341!! <code>Kh_bg_xy</code> for the tension (h-points) and shear (q-points) components
4342!! respectively.
4343!!
4344!! The full viscosity includes the dynamic components as follows:
4345!! \f[
4346!! \kappa_h(x,y,t) = r(\Delta,L_d)
4347!! \max \left( \kappa_{static}, \kappa_{Sm}, \kappa_{Lth} \right)
4348!! \f]
4349!! where \f$r(\Delta,L_d)\f$ is a resolution function.
4350!!
4351!! The dynamic Smagorinsky and Leith viscosity schemes are exclusive with each
4352!! other.
4353!!
4354!! \subsection section_viscous_boundary_conditions Viscous boundary conditions
4355!!
4356!! Free slip boundary conditions have been coded, although no slip boundary
4357!! conditions can be used with the Laplacian viscosity based on the 2D land-sea
4358!! mask. For a western boundary, for example, the boundary conditions with the
4359!! biharmonic operator would be written as:
4360!! \f[
4361!! \partial_x v = 0 ; \partial_x^3 v = 0 ; u = 0 ; \partial_x^2 u = 0 ,
4362!! \f]
4363!! while for a Laplacian operator, they are simply
4364!! \f[
4365!! \partial_x v = 0 ; u = 0 .
4366!! \f]
4367!! These boundary conditions are largely dictated by the use of an Arakawa
4368!! C-grid and by the varying layer thickness.
4369!!
4370!! \subsection section_anisotropic_viscosity Anisotropic viscosity
4371!!
4372!! \cite Large2001 proposed enhancing viscosity in a particular direction and the
4373!! approach was generalized in \cite Smith2003. We use the second form of their
4374!! two coefficient anisotropic viscosity (section 4.3). We also replace their
4375!! \f$A^\prime\f$ and $D$ such that \f$2A^\prime = 2 \kappa_h + D\f$ and
4376!! \f$\kappa_a = D\f$ so that \f$\kappa_h\f$ can be considered the isotropic
4377!! viscosity and \f$\kappa_a=D\f$ can be consider the anisotropic viscosity. The
4378!! direction of anisotropy is defined by a unit vector \f$\hat{\bf
4379!! n}=(n_1,n_2)\f$.
4380!!
4381!! The contributions to the stress tensor are
4382!! \f[
4383!! \begin{pmatrix}
4384!! \sigma_T \\\\ \sigma_S
4385!! \end{pmatrix}
4386!! =
4387!! \left[
4388!! \begin{pmatrix}
4389!! 2 \kappa_h + \kappa_a & 0 \\\\
4390!! 0 & 2 \kappa_h
4391!! \end{pmatrix}
4392!! + 2 \kappa_a n_1 n_2
4393!! \begin{pmatrix}
4394!! - 2 n_1 n_2 & n_1^2 - n_2^2 \\\\
4395!! n_1^2 - n_2^2 & 2 n_1 n_2
4396!! \end{pmatrix}
4397!! \right]
4398!! \begin{pmatrix}
4399!! \dot{e}_T \\\\ \dot{e}_S
4400!! \end{pmatrix}
4401!! \f]
4402!! Dissipation of kinetic energy requires \f$\kappa_h \geq 0\f$ and \f$2 \kappa_h + \kappa_a \geq 0\f$.
4403!! Note that when anisotropy is aligned with the x-direction, \f$n_1 = \pm 1\f$, then
4404!! \f$n_2 = 0\f$ and the cross terms vanish. The accelerations in this aligned limit
4405!! with constant coefficients become
4406!! \f{eqnarray*}{
4407!! \hat{\bf x} \cdot \nabla \cdot {\bf \sigma}
4408!! & = &
4409!! \partial_x \left( \left( \kappa_h + \frac{1}{2} \kappa_a \right) \dot{e}_T \right)
4410!! + \partial_y \left( \kappa_h \dot{e}_S \right)
4411!! \\\\
4412!! & = &
4413!! \left( \kappa_h + \kappa_a \right) \partial_{xx} u
4414!! + \kappa_h \partial_{yy} u
4415!! - \frac{1}{2} \kappa_a \partial_x \left( \partial_x u + \partial_y v \right)
4416!! \\\\
4417!! \hat{\bf y} \cdot \nabla \cdot {\bf \sigma}
4418!! & = &
4419!! \partial_x \left( \kappa_h \dot{e}_S \right)
4420!! - \partial_y \left( \left( \kappa_h + \frac{1}{2} \kappa_a \right) \dot{e}_T \right)
4421!! \\\\
4422!! & = &
4423!! \kappa_h \partial_{xx} v
4424!! + \left( \kappa_h + \kappa_a \right) \partial_{yy} v
4425!! - \frac{1}{2} \kappa_a \partial_y \left( \partial_x u + \partial_y v \right)
4426!! \f}
4427!! which has contributions akin to a negative divergence damping (a divergence
4428!! enhancement?) but which is weaker than the enhanced tension terms by half.
4429!!
4430!! \subsection section_viscous_discretization Discretization
4431!!
4432!! The horizontal tension, \f$\dot{e}_T\f$, is stored in variable <code>sh_xx</code> and
4433!! discretized as
4434!! \f[
4435!! \dot{e}_T
4436!! = \frac{\Delta y}{\Delta x} \delta_i \left( \frac{1}{\Delta y} u \right)
4437!! - \frac{\Delta x}{\Delta y} \delta_j \left( \frac{1}{\Delta x} v \right)
4438!! .
4439!! \f]
4440!! The horizontal divergent strain, \f$\dot{e}_D\f$, is stored in variable
4441!! <code>div_xx</code> and discretized as
4442!! \f[
4443!! \dot{e}_D
4444!! = \frac{1}{h A} \left( \delta_i \left( \overline{h}^i \Delta y \, u \right)
4445!! + \delta_j \left( \overline{h}^j\Delta x \, v \right) \right)
4446!! .
4447!! \f]
4448!! Note that for expediency this is the exact discretization used in the
4449!! continuity equation.
4450!!
4451!! The horizontal shear strain, \f$\dot{e}_S\f$, is stored in variable <code>sh_xy</code>
4452!! and discretized as
4453!! \f[
4454!! \dot{e}_S = v_x + u_y
4455!! \f]
4456!! where
4457!! \f{align*}{
4458!! v_x &= \frac{\Delta y}{\Delta x} \delta_i \left( \frac{1}{\Delta y} v \right) \\\\
4459!! u_y &= \frac{\Delta x}{\Delta y} \delta_j \left( \frac{1}{\Delta x} u \right)
4460!! \f}
4461!! which are calculated separately so that no-slip or free-slip boundary
4462!! conditions can be applied to \f$v_x\f$ and \f$u_y\f$ where appropriate.
4463!!
4464!! The tendency for the x-component of the divergence of stress is stored in
4465!! variable <code>diffu</code> and discretized as
4466!! \f[
4467!! \hat{\bf x} \cdot \left( \nabla \cdot {\bf \sigma} \right) =
4468!! \frac{1}{A \overline{h}^i} \left(
4469!! \frac{1}{\Delta y} \delta_i \left( h \Delta y^2 \kappa_h \dot{e}_T \right) +
4470!! \frac{1}{\Delta x} \delta_j \left( \tilde{h}^{ij} \Delta x^2 \kappa_h \dot{e}_S \right)
4471!! \right)
4472!! .
4473!! \f]
4474!!
4475!! The tendency for the y-component of the divergence of stress is stored in
4476!! variable <code>diffv</code> and discretized as
4477!! \f[
4478!! \hat{\bf y} \cdot \left( \nabla \cdot {\bf \sigma} \right) =
4479!! \frac{1}{A \overline{h}^j} \left(
4480!! \frac{1}{\Delta y} \delta_i \left( \tilde{h}^{ij} \Delta y^2 A_M \dot{e}_S \right)
4481!! - \frac{1}{\Delta x} \delta_j \left( h \Delta x^2 A_M \dot{e}_T \right)
4482!! \right)
4483!! .
4484!! \f]
4485!!
